Remarkably, it decisively outperforms multi-view baselines, yielding absolute success rate improvements of 9.4% on LIBERO-Long and 17.5% in real-world long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [135] [Is Forward Prediction Enough? Physical State Grounding for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06799)
*Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Mingyuan Jia,Ruiqing Yin,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Junfeng Li,Jinxuan Lu,Hengtao Li,Tianran Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Wen Chen,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PSG-JEPA是一种物理约束的世界模型，通过在训练阶段添加两个额外的接地目标（个体隐变量与本体感受状态的映射、隐变量对与关节角变化的映射），显著提升了隐变量的可识别性，并在隐变量探查、目标条件规划和策略学习三个层面均超越了现有最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前JEPA-based世界模型的前向预测目标未能显式强制个体隐变量可靠地识别机器人中心物理状态，或从隐变量对中提取状态变化，这限制了下游规划和策略性能。

Method: PSG-JEPA在标准JEPA前向预测基础上，引入两个互补的接地目标：(1)将个体隐变量接地到机器人本体感受状态；(2)将隐变量对接地到多时间尺度的关节角变化。两个目标仅在训练阶段应用，不改变推理架构和计算成本。

Result: 在三个层级的实验中——(1)通过探查评估隐变量可识别性；(2)基于冻结隐变量的目标条件规划；(3)仿真和真实机器人上的策略学习——PSG-JEPA均一致性地超越最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过在训练过程中引入物理接地目标，PSG-JEPA成功塑造了具有更强可识别性的隐变量空间，在不增加推理开销的前提下，显著提升了世界模型在机器人规划和策略学习中的性能表现。

Abstract: Learning structured and control-relevant latent representations remains a key challenge for world models. Recent JEPA-based world models learn action-conditioned predictive latent dynamics from observation sequences. However, their forward-prediction objectives do not explicitly enforce reliable identifiability of robot-centric physical state from individual latents or state changes from latent pairs, which can limit downstream planning and policy performance. We propose PSG-JEPA, a physically grounded JEPA world model that shapes its latent space with two complementary grounding objectives beyond forward prediction: grounding individual latents in robot proprioceptive state, and grounding latent pairs in multi-horizon joint-angle changes. Both objectives are applied only during training, leaving the inference architecture and computational cost unchanged. To comprehensively evaluate PSG-JEPA, we conduct experiments at three levels: (1) latent identifiability via probing, (2) goal-conditioned planning on frozen latents, and (3) policy learning in simulation and on a real robot. Experiments demonstrate that our PSG-JEPA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art latent world-model baselines at all three levels.

</details>


### [136] [R2S-EGO: Dual-Proxy Refinement for Sparse-Capture Real-to-Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06827)
*Shuai Fang,Xin Deng,Yuchen Kang,Zhenjiang Li,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2S-EGO是一种新的真实到仿真(R2S)方法，通过结合机器人代理和几何代理，利用相机控制合成技术填补缺失视角，在三个Replica场景的48个Unitree G1机器人视角中达到19.062 dB PSNR，真实G1坐姿成功率达82.5%±6.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统真实到仿真(R2S)方法依赖场景表示来渲染机器人轨迹观测，但存在以下问题：1)密集多视角捕获限制了每个环境真实图像捕获效率；2)稀疏的人类捕获导致行为范围内的机器人视角缺乏支持。相机控制合成可以填补缺失视角，但需要行为可接受的查询和捕获锚定的结构条件。

Method: R2S-EGO方法包含三个核心组件：1)仿真器导出的机器人代理，表示行为范围的可执行查询域；2)捕获锚定的几何代理，提供场景特定的结构条件；3)固定预算选择机制，针对当前支持不足但几何支持可用的区域进行优化。生成的观测作为伪观测整合以优化视觉资产，同时保持真实捕获为锚点。融合的几何代理还提供场景碰撞表面，并在轮次间刷新，在保持机器人动力学和控制栈固定的同时精化现有仿真场景。

Result: 在48个冻结的Unitree G1机器人视角和三个Replica场景中，六视角R2S-EGO达到19.062 dB PSNR，相比最强R2S基线方法的14.226 dB有显著提升。在五个配对策略训练种子中，R2S-EGO实现82.5%±6.8%的真实G1坐姿成功率，而GaussGym仅为10.0%±10.5%。

Conclusion: R2S-EGO通过创新性地将机器人代理与几何代理相结合，有效解决了真实到仿真中的视角缺失问题，在渲染质量和策略性能方面均取得了显著突破，为机器人仿真训练提供了更高效、更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-to-sim (R2S) depends on scene representations that render observations along robot ego trajectories, yet dense multi-view capture limits per-environment real-image capture-count efficiency, and sparse human capture can
  leave behavior-scoped robot views under-supported. Camera-controlled synthesis can fill missing views, but its use in R2S requires behavior-admissible queries and capture-anchored structural conditioning. We present R2S-EGO,
  which couples a simulator-derived robot proxy that represents the behavior-scoped executable query domain with a capture-anchored geometry proxy that supplies scene-specific structural conditions. Within this domain, fixed-
  budget selection targets current support deficits for which geometry support is available. The generated observations are assimilated as pseudo-observations to refine the visual asset, while real captures remain anchors. The
  fused geometry proxy also supplies the scene collision surface, which is refreshed between rounds. Together, these updates refine the existing simulation scene while its robot dynamics and control stack stay fixed. Across 48
  frozen Unitree G1 ego views in three Replica scenes, six-view R2S-EGO reaches 19.062 dB PSNR, compared with 14.226 dB for the strongest reported R2S baseline. Across five paired policy-training seeds, R2S-EGO achieves 82.5%
  +/- 6.8% real-G1 sitting success, compared with 10.0% +/- 10.5% for GaussGym.

</details>


### [137] [When Coordination Becomes a Threat: Communication Attacks in LLM-Controlled Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06830)
*Zhen Huang,Zhihuang Liu,Weijia Shi,Yifan Yang,Weishang Wu,Zhiping Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM驱动的协同多机器人系统中的通信安全风险，发现不安全信息会转化为不安全行为，并提出CPV Gate防御机制可将违规率从70.0%降至36.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为规划器在多机器人系统中的应用日益广泛，但现有研究主要聚焦单体机器人安全，对多机器人协作中的通信风险认识不足。已有研究仅限于DMAS架构的初步分析，尚不清楚这些风险是否在其他常见通信架构中持续存在，以及攻击者访问设置如何影响风险传播。

Method: (1) 构建两种通信攻击：外部入口攻击和特权系统内攻击，对应不同攻击者访问场景；(2) 在DMAS、HMAS-1、HMAS-2三种架构上评估攻击效果；(3) 使用三种LLM和五个具身多机器人任务进行实验；(4) 提出声明溯源与验证（CPV）Gate防御机制，在下游复用前验证通信声明。

Result: 不安全信息可转化为不安全行为，且该风险存在于所有三种架构：DMAS达到96.7%的入口认可率和100%的认可后激活率；HMAS-1达到97.8%的不安全行为成功率；HMAS-2触发88.3%的任务定义不安全行为槽位。CPV Gate机制将违规率从70.0%降低至36.6%。

Conclusion: 基于通信的攻击对LLM驱动的多机器人系统构成严重安全风险，且该风险在DMAS、HMAS-1和HMAS-2架构中均普遍存在。CPV Gate机制提供部分缓解但无法完全消除漏洞，仍需进一步研究更全面的防御策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as high-level planners in embodied multi-robot systems, enabling robots to interpret natural language instructions and coordinate executable actions. Yet, this growing reliance on LLM planners also raises security concerns. Prior work has focused mainly on individual robots, while communication risks in multi-robot collaboration remain insufficiently understood. Existing multi-robot studies are further limited to preliminary analysis under the Decentralized Multi-agent System (DMAS) architecture, so it remains unclear whether these risks persist across other common communication architectures and how attacker access settings shape their propagation. To fill this gap, we formulate two communication attacks corresponding to distinct attacker access settings: the External Entry Point Attack and the Privileged In-System Attack. We evaluate both attacks across DMAS, HMAS-1, and HMAS-2 using three LLMs and five embodied multi-robot tasks. Results show that unsafe information can turn into unsafe actions across all three architectures: DMAS reaches a 96.7\% entry endorsement rate and a 100\% post endorsement activation rate, HMAS-1 reaches a 97.8\% unsafe action success rate, and HMAS-2 triggers 88.3\% of task defined unsafe action slots. To mitigate risks from trusted information flow, we introduce the Claim Provenance and Verification (CPV) Gate, which verifies communicated claims before downstream reuse and reduces the violation rate from 70.0\% to 36.6\%.

</details>


### [138] [Unordered Landmark Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06833)
*Hao Ren,Junzhe Zhu,Yihan Li,Zetong Bi,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Yiqing Yuan,Zhaoliang Wan,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ULVN框架，这是一种仅使用RGB图像的无序地标视觉导航系统，无需时间序列或里程计先验，能够直接从不结构化图像构建拓扑地图并实现高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像目标导航是具身AI的基本能力，但现有方法严重依赖时间有序的视频流或辅助传感器（深度、LiDAR）来维持空间一致性。这种时序和多模态依赖严重限制了可扩展性，特别是在使用众包或预录制的无序图像集合部署机器人时。当移除时间先验后，当前方法会面临严重的感知混淆、噪声关联和灾难性建图失败等问题。

Method: 提出ULVN统一RGB-only框架，通过集成建图、定位和规划来系统性地缓解误差累积。具体包括：1）通过校准几何验证和最大生成树优化从不结构化图像直接构建鲁棒的2D拓扑地图；2）放弃顺序启发式方法，采用基于图的信念传播滤波器和熵自适应融合技术实现全局定位和动态子目标规划。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界部署中的广泛实验表明，ULVN显著优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 该方法成功消除了对时序和多模态依赖的需求，实现了对无序图像集合的有效利用，为具身AI导航的可扩展性部署提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation is a fundamental capability for embodied AI, yet its practical deployment is strained by strong prior assumptions. Existing methods predominantly rely on temporally ordered video streams or auxiliary sensors (e.g., depth, LiDAR) to maintain spatial consistency. These sequential and multimodal dependencies severely restrict scalability, especially when deploying robots using crowd-sourced or pre-recorded unordered image collections. When temporal priors are removed, current methods struggle with severe perceptual aliasing, noisy associations, and catastrophic mapping failures. To address this underexplored challenge, we propose Unordered Landmark Visual Navigation (ULVN), a unified RGB-only framework free from temporal and odometric priors. ULVN systematically mitigates error accumulation by integrating mapping, localization, and planning. Specifically, it constructs a robust 2D topological map directly from unstructured images via calibrated geometric verification and maximum spanning forest refinement. For closed-loop execution, ULVN abandons sequential heuristics, utilizing a graph-based belief propagation filter with entropy-adaptive fusion for global localization and dynamic subgoal planning. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world deployments demonstrate that ULVN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [139] [Are Visual Place Recognition Models Recognizing Places or Conditions? Distractor-Augmented Evaluation and Condition Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06847)
*Beomsu Kim,Minwoo Jung,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对长期视觉地点识别（VPR）中因众包数据库混杂不同条件图像导致的干扰问题，提出干扰增强召回（DAR）指标以量化干扰影响，并引入条件抑制技术（INLP和LEACE）去除描述子中的条件信息。实验表明，DAR排名与标准召回率不同，条件抑制能提高DAR而不降低标准性能，证明干扰鲁棒性与标准检索性能不同且可通过条件抑制改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包地图数据库可能混杂不同条件图像，包含与查询条件相似但地点不同的干扰图像。现有VPR方法可能因描述子编码了光照、天气、季节等条件信息，而依据条件相似性而非地点身份进行检索，导致性能受干扰影响。

Method: 提出干扰增强召回（DAR）指标，用于隔离并量化干扰的影响；提出条件抑制方法（INLP和LEACE），从VPR描述子中去除条件信息。

Result: 在11种方法和6个数据集上评估，DAR@1下的方法排名与Recall@1（R@1）不同；应用INLP和LEACE进行条件抑制通常能提高DAR@1而不降低R@1。

Conclusion: 干扰鲁棒性与标准检索性能不同，且可通过抑制条件信息来改善。

Abstract: Long-term Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is typically evaluated by matching queries from one condition against a database from another. Crowdsourced map databases, however, may mix conditions and include images that resemble the query in condition but depict different places. In the presence of these distractors, a method may retrieve by condition similarity rather than place identity. We argue that this susceptibility arises because the discriminability of VPR methods allows them to encode information such as illumination, weather, and seasonal appearance in their descriptors. We therefore introduce Distractor-Augmented Recall (DAR) to isolate and quantify the effect of distractors, and propose condition suppression to remove condition information from VPR descriptors. Across eleven methods and six datasets, method rankings under DAR@1 differ from those under Recall@1 (R@1), while applying INLP and LEACE as condition suppression methods generally improves DAR@1 without reducing R@1. Thus, distractor robustness is distinct from standard retrieval performance and can be improved by suppressing condition information.

</details>


### [140] [How Should I Pick a Foundation Model for My Robot? In Favor of a Community Evaluation Framework for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06898)
*Eric Nichols,Alva Markelius,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对社会机器人应用中基础模型选择的难题，提出五个评估维度（会话能力、用户安全、具身角色、目标场景有效性、受众适宜性）和三层评估漏斗范式（通用指标→模拟交互→机器人特定评估），旨在降低模型选择成本并提高决策质量，呼吁社区共同构建评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者在构建基于基础模型的社会机器人应用时面临模型选择的困难：公开排行榜对实时具身社会交互的指导作用有限，而大规模直接评估需要稀缺的参与者、机器人和实验者时间，成本高昂且不可行。需要更经济高效的模型评估和选择方法。

Method: 提出五个评估维度和三层评估漏斗范式：(1)识别五个关键评估维度——会话能力、用户安全、具身角色、目标场景有效性、受众适宜性；(2)构建三层评估漏斗——第一层使用通用指标进行初步筛选，第二层扩展到模拟交互测试，第三层进行成本更高的机器人特定评估；(3)将五个维度映射到三个评估层级，梳理现有评估方法的覆盖情况和缺失领域。

Result: 成功定义了适用于社会机器人基础模型评估的五维框架，设计了从低成本到高分辨率的渐进式三层评估流程，系统性地识别了各维度在各评估层级的方法现状和空白点，为模型选择提供了结构化的评估路径。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套系统的社会机器人基础模型评估体系，通过五维度和三层漏斗的框架，为解决模型选择难题提供了可行方案。研究强调需要社区协作来完善评估框架，填补现有方法空白，最终实现更高效、更可靠的社会机器人模型评估与选择。

Abstract: Researchers who seek to build social robot applications on foundation models are faced with a difficult question: how should we pick a model? Public leaderboards offer little guidance: the demands of real-time, embodied social interaction lie largely outside their focus. And direct evaluation is impractical at scale: each embodied study requires scarce participant, robot, and experimenter time. In this paper, we identify five evaluation dimensions for foundation models in social robots: (i) conversational competence, (ii) user safety, (iii) embodied character, (iv) target scene effectiveness, and (v) audience appropriateness. To make model selection cheaper and better informed, we propose a three-tiered evaluation funnel paradigm that first filters with general metrics, then extends to simulated interactions, and terminates in more expensive, robot-specific evaluation. We map all five dimensions across all three tiers, chart where applicable evaluation methods exist and are missing, and close with a call to action: let's build the evaluation framework together as a community.

</details>


### [141] [Spatiotemporal Agility: Time-Constrained Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Guided Dynamic Quadrupedal Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06907)
*Yidong Zhu,Zibo Dai,Tongning Zhang,Leixin Chang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合视觉着陆点预测与直接位置/时间条件化RL运动策略的四足机器人动态接球框架，系统级集成了多相机感知、在线轨迹预测、低延迟目标通信和sim-to-real运动控制，实验表明相比速度追踪基线方法具有更高接球成功率和更优的sim-to-real性能转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人需要在约束时间内感知并与复杂动态环境交互，现有基于速度追踪策略的四足运动工作难以在严格时间约束内精确到达目标；实时感知与敏捷运动整合因传感器延迟和处理延迟而具有挑战性。本文通过引入具有挑战性的动态接球任务来具体研究和基准化此类敏捷性。

Method: 提出一个集成框架：(1) 视觉模块用于着陆点和时间预测；(2) 直接位置和条件化的RL运动策略（替代中间速度指令）；系统级贡献包括完成实时机器人拦截系统的闭环部署流水线，集成多相机感知、在线轨迹预测、低延迟目标通信和sim-to-real运动控制。通过显式预测未来时空目标来减轻动态拦截期间的感知延迟。

Result: 进行了大量四足机器人接球实验。与速度追踪基线对比实验表明：在预测着陆点2米内、飞行时间0.8-1.2秒的条件下，直接目标条件化方法实现了更高的接球成功率；部署后策略表现出较小的性能差距，表明在这些试验中sim-to-real行为有所改善。

Conclusion: 直接目标条件化方法有效减轻了动态拦截期间的感知延迟，机器人在测试设置下成功完成了动态接球任务。该方法在四足机器人敏捷运动控制方面展现了优越性，特别是在需要精确时空目标的动态交互场景中。

Abstract: Legged robots require robust agility to perceive and interact with complex and dynamic environments within a constrained time. However, most existing quadruped locomotion works rely on velocity-tracking policy, which struggle to reach precise targets within strict temporal constraints. Moreover, integrating real-time perception with agile locomotion for highly dynamic targets remains challenging due to sensor latency and processing delays. To concretely study and benchmark such agility in dynamic settings, we introduce a challenging ball-catching task for legged robots. This paper proposes an integrated framework that combines a vision module for landing point and time prediction with a direct position and time conditioned RL locomotion policy, instead of intermediate velocity commands. Beyond the method design, this work presents a system-level contribution that completes real-time robotic interception system that integrates multi-camera perception, online trajectory prediction, low-latency target communication, and sim-to-real locomotion control into a closed-loop deployment pipeline. By explicitly predicting the future spatial-temporal target, our approach mitigates perception latency during dynamic interception. We conducted extensive ball-catching experiments for the legged robot. Through comparative experiments against a velocity-tracking baseline, our direct target-conditioned approach achieves a higher success rate in catching balls with predicted landing spots within 2 meters and flight times between 0.8 and 1.2 seconds. This shows that the robot has successfully completed the dynamic ball-catching task under our tested setup. Furthermore, our policy exhibits a smaller performance gap after deployment, suggesting improved sim-to-real behavior in these trials.

</details>


### [142] [Cross-View Action Consistency for Camera-Robust Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06965)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Xuan Wang,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在相机移动时性能下降的问题，提出一种仅依赖场景RGB、语言和本体感知的动作流速度场正则化方法。通过构造动作等价的视图对进行训练，在LIBERO-Plus数据集上显著提升相机扰动下的成功率（87.2%，较基线提升7.4个百分点），并在真实机器人上验证了有效性（成功率从53.3%提升至74.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从固定场景相机微调的VLA策略在相机位置变化时容易失效，即使任务、物体、语言和机器人状态保持不变。需要提高策略对场景相机视角变化的鲁棒性，同时避免依赖额外的相机标签、外参、深度或点云输入。

Method: 仅使用场景RGB图像、语言和本体感知作为输入，遮挡腕部视觉流以防止未受扰动的视觉捷径干扰。对于基于流的VLA，提出正则化动作流速度场（即直接积分生成连续动作块的量）。通过重置原始LIBERO演示到相同MuJoCo状态并渲染标称和扰动场景相机视图，构造动作等价的视图对。两视图均由流匹配监督，交叉视图损失鼓励相同采样流坐标处预测的动作流速度一致。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus相机扰动轨道上，方法达到87.2±0.4%成功率（每种子4797次rollout，3个训练种子），较相同配对数据上的纯流匹配训练（79.8±0.8%）提升7.4个百分点，较朴素混合相机SFT提升12.5个百分点，同时保持标称相机ID性能（95.0±0.8%）。打乱配对控制实验成功率降至25.8%，表明增益依赖于动作等价配对。在真实机器人上测试三个桌面任务（每任务10次rollout和相机放置），保留相机成功率从53.3%提升至74.4%，使用相同的单场景RGB推理接口。

Conclusion: 提出的动作流速度场正则化方法有效提升了VLA策略对相机视角变化的鲁棒性，无需额外输入信息。实验验证了动作等价配对对性能增益的关键作用，在真实机器人上取得了显著改善，表明该方法能够增强策略在实际部署中的适应性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies fine-tuned from a fixed scene camera can fail when the camera is moved, even when the task, objects, language, and robot state are unchanged. We study scene-camera viewpoint robustness using only a scene RGB image, language, and proprioception, without camera labels, extrinsics, depth, or point-cloud inputs. The wrist stream is masked throughout to prevent an unperturbed visual shortcut from confounding attribution to scene-camera variation. For flow-based VLAs, we propose to regularize the action-flow velocity field, the quantity directly integrated to generate continuous action chunks. We construct action-equivalent view pairs by resetting original LIBERO demonstrations to the same MuJoCo state and rendering nominal and perturbed scene-camera views. Both views are supervised by flow matching, while a cross-view loss encourages their predicted action-flow velocities to agree at the same sampled flow coordinates. On the LIBERO-Plus camera-perturbation track, our method reaches 87.2$\pm$0.4% (4,797 rollouts per seed across 3 training seeds), +7.4pp over flow-matching-only training on the same paired data (79.8$\pm$0.8%, also 3 seeds) and +12.5pp over naive mixed-camera SFT, while maintaining nominal-camera ID performance (95.0$\pm$0.8%; same-data FM-only: 95.0$\pm$4.3%). A shuffled-pair control collapses to 25.8%, showing that the gain depends on action-equivalent pairing. On a real robot, we evaluate three tabletop tasks with 10 rollouts per task and camera placement; held-out-camera success improves from 53.3% to 74.4% under the same single-scene-RGB inference interface.

</details>


### [143] [Exact Thrust-Reversal Limits of Bidirectional Propellers under Bounded Motor Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06991)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了双向螺旋桨推力反转的内在限制：线性推力反转（斜率非零）需要无限大的电机输入，导致电机电流/电压峰值和推力跟踪性能下降；而高阶反转则可避免奇异电机命令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向螺旋桨常被简化为有符号推力源，但其推力实际是转子速度的符号二次函数。推力反转必须经过零转子速度，此时有界电机电矩改变推力的能力崩溃。研究这一障碍对于理解空中机器人的力控制、加速度控制和交互控制参考至关重要。

Method: 在预定义平滑度的有界电机输入下，研究精确推力轨迹可重复性。推导归一化推力坐标模型（零推力时输入增益消失），证明基于期望推力过零阶数的充分必要条件，推导DC电机的电流和电压正则性要求，并通过电机-螺旋桨实验验证。

Result: 1) 线性推力反转（非零斜率过零）需要无界电机输入；2) 高阶反转可避免奇异电机命令；3) 实验证实线性反转出现局部电流/电压峰值和推力跟踪退化，高阶反转无此问题；4) 提供了设计避免奇异电机命令的推力反转的直接设计规则。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了内在的致动器级限制，必须在空中机器人的力控制、加速度控制和交互控制参考中予以考虑。该发现对飞行器的精确控制设计和性能优化具有指导意义。

Abstract: Bidirectional propellers are often treated as signed thrust sources, but their thrust is a signed-quadratic function of rotor speed.Thus, thrust reversal necessarily occurs through zero rotor speed, where the ability of a bounded motor torque to change thrust collapses.This work formalizes this obstruction by studying exact thrust-trajectory reproducibility under bounded motor inputs with prescribed smoothness.We derive a normalized thrust-coordinate model with vanishing input gain at zero thrust, and prove necessary and sufficient reproducibility conditions in terms of the zero-crossing order of the desired thrust.Generic reversals, in which thrust crosses zero with nonzero slope, require unbounded motor input; the resulting conditions provide direct design rules for shaping thrust reversals that avoid singular motor commands.We also derive the corresponding current and voltage regularity requirements for a DC motor driving a bidirectional propeller.Experiments on a motor-propeller setup validate the predicted reversal-order effects, showing localized current/voltage peaks and thrust-tracking degradation for linear reversals, but not for higher-order reversals.These results expose an intrinsic actuator-level limitation that must be considered in force, acceleration, and interaction-control references for aerial robots.

</details>


### [144] [Decoupling Intention from Trajectory: A Representational Deduction Framework for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06994)
*Xiangkai Ma,Yue Ma,Junjie Wang,Sheng Xu,Mingyang Li,Han Zhang,Yuzheng Zhuang,Wenzhong Li,Zhihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本摘要介绍了一种名为PILOT的新方法，通过引入表征演绎（RD）和运动思维链（CoT）指导，解决了现有世界行动模型中视觉分支无法捕捉世界状态转移信息的问题。RD显式建模状态转移令牌，作为推理空间中的CoT来引导细粒度运动轨迹生成。实验显示，RD提升了世界行动模型在复杂机器人任务中的成功率和泛化能力，增强了物理可解释性，并通过丰富的状态转移监督缓解了稀疏监督问题，使其成为高效的少样本微调策略，并具有良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界行动模型（WAMs）中的视觉分支主要预测静态视觉观测，无法反映运动交互下世界状态演变的潜在转移信息。这导致行动模型中高层物理条件演化与低层动作轨迹生成之间的表征纠缠，形成结构瓶颈，削弱了世界演化建模对动作生成的预测能力。

Method: 提出PILOT（物理推理潜在优化轨迹），其核心表征演绎（RD）通过整合运动思维链（CoT）指导作为原生模型能力来弥合这一差距。RD旨在鼓励行动分支显式建模潜在状态转移令牌，这些令牌作为CoT保留在推理空间中，以指导细粒度运动轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，RD不仅显著提高了世界行动模型在复杂机器人操作任务中的成功率和泛化能力，还通过解耦高层运动语义与低层轨迹细节增强了模型的可解释性。此外，RD引入的丰富状态转移监督信号有效缓解了动作生成中的稀疏监督问题，使其成为高效的少样本真实机器人微调策略，并展现出向主流世界行动模型架构迁移的优越可扩展性。

Conclusion: PILOT及其核心RD有效解决了现有世界行动模型的局限性，通过引入转移信息提升了模型性能、可解释性和可扩展性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) aim to construct a unified architecture capable of understanding world state evolution and guiding to generative motion planning. However, existing visual branches focus on predicting static visual observation, rather than reflecting potential transition information that captures the evolution of world states under motion interactions. This leads to representational entanglement between high-level physical condition evolution and low-level action trajectory generation within the Action Model, creating a structural bottleneck while weakening the predictive capability of world evolution modeling for action generation. We propose PILOT (Physical Inference for Latent Optimized Trajectories), whose core Representational Deduction (RD) bridges this gap by integrating motion thought-of-chain (CoT) guidance as a native model capability. Specifically, RD aims to encourage the action branch to explicitly model potential state transition tokens, which are retained as CoT in the reasoning space to guide fine-grained motion trajectory. Experiments demonstrate that RD not only significantly improves the success rate and generalization ability of WAMs in complex robotic manipulation tasks but also enhances the model's physical interpretability by decoupling high-level motion semantics from low-level trajectory details. Furthermore, the abundant state transition supervision signals introduced by RD effectively alleviate the sparse supervision in action generation, enabling it to serve as an efficient few-shot real-robot fine-tuning strategy and demonstrating superior scalability for migration to mainstream WAM architectures.

</details>


### [145] [Automated Terminal-to-Housing Assembly System for Flat Ribbon Cable Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06996)
*Eunkyu Choi,Joonho Seo,Seungmin Lee,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于双向单排扁平带状电缆线束(FRCH)的传感极简自动化装配系统，通过纯机械序列实现装配，无需主动感知或视觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRCH装配涉及柔性且密集的几何约束下机械耦合的多端子插入，与传统的销孔配合或单端子插入任务不同，具有更高的技术挑战性。

Method: 系统通过四个纯机械步骤实现装配：电缆对齐、倾斜滑动、编织和夹紧。每个机制逐步减少相关性端子错位、插入干涉和不稳定性，最终锁定。

Result: 在80次试验中，端到端过程成功率为83.75%，前半部分和后半部分成功率分别为85.0%和82.5%。半速运行周期时间为33秒。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个针对多针座FRCH端子-座体装配的自动化原型，在无传感器和无视觉条件下实现了高效的机械装配。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensor-minimal automated assembly system for bidirectional single-row flat ribbon cable harnesses (FRCHs). Unlike conventional peg-in-hole or single-terminal insertion tasks, FRCH assembly involves mechanically coupled multi-terminal insertion under flexible and dense geometric constraints. To address this problem, the proposed system performs the assembly through a purely mechanical sequence consisting of Cable Alignment, Lean & Slide, Weaving, and Clamping, without relying on active sensing or vision. Each mechanism is designed to progressively reduce correlated terminal misalignment, insertion interference, and instability before final locking. Experiments on bidirectional single-row FRCHs achieved an 83.75% end-to-end process success rate over 80 trials, with success rates of 85.0% and 82.5% in the first and second halves, respectively. The cycle time was 33 s under half-speed operation. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first automated prototype for FRCH terminal-to-housing assembly for multi-pin housings.

</details>


### [146] [A Haptic Robot Finger Designed for Guqin Instrument Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07002)
*Tianwei Zhang,Hanming Yan,Yang Yang. Ziya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文设计了一种高精度触觉传感仿生手指，专门用于演奏古琴等需要精细触觉感知的传统乐器，成功实现了弦接触任务的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着类人机器人和具身智能技术的快速发展，虽然已出现多种乐器演奏机器人，但这些系统主要采用开环位置控制方式，无法处理需要灵巧双手和精确触觉感知的乐器演奏任务，如小提琴、吉他和古琴等中国传统乐器。这限制了机器人在文化遗产保护和文化传播领域的应用潜力。

Method: 研究人员设计并开发了一种仿生多模态触觉指尖，通过模仿人类手指指尖和指甲的形状结构，实现了高精密度的触觉感知功能。该方法将触觉感知技术与机器人技术相结合，在古琴这一具有挑战性的验证场景上进行了系统测试，包括空弦与按音比较、泛音调音以及触觉触发的双手协调等多个关键任务。

Result: 成功在古琴上验证了仿生触觉手指的性能，包括：(1)准确区分空弦和按音的触觉差异；(2)实现泛音的精确调音；(3)完成触觉触发的双手协调操作。这些结果表明所设计的触觉传感系统能够有效支持复杂的乐器演奏任务。

Conclusion: 本研究通过将触觉感知与机器人技术深度融合，不仅解决了传统乐器演奏中的关键技术难题，更为世界遗产保护和文化传播提供了新的技术路径。研究成果具有重要的学术价值和应用前景，特别是在促进传统文化的数字化传承方面展现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of humanoid robotics and embodied intelligence technologies, numerous musical instrument-playing robots have emerged in recent years, such as pianos, chime bells, and taiko drums. These robots primarily employ open-loop positional control, rendering them incapable of operating instruments requiring dexterous hands and precise tactile perception, such as a violin, guitar, and guqin. This paper describes the design and validation of a high-precision tactile-sensing finger. By mimicking the shape of the fingertip and fingernail found on a human finger, we develop a biomimetic multimodal haptic fingertip and validate it on selected guqin string-contact tasks, including open-string and stopped-note comparisons, harmonic-tuning, and tactile-triggered bimanual coordination, using the guqin, a traditional Chinese musical instrument, as a challenging validation scenario rather than as a fully demonstrated robotic performance system. This research integrates tactile sensing with robotics technology, thereby contributing to applications in world heritage conservation and cultural dissemination.

</details>


### [147] [Benchmarking and Reasoning Distillation of Large Language Models for Feedback Controller Design in Complex Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07004)
*Zhongchao Zhou,Yixuan Xie,Wenwei Yu,Yuxi Lu,Yaonan Zhu,Qian Niu,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDyControlBench基准，评估大语言模型在复杂反馈控制器设计任务上的表现，并开发轻量边缘部署模型，验证其在实际机器人臂上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科学领域表现出色，但反馈控制器设计领域研究不足；现有基准仅关注线性单自由度系统和大型API模型，缺乏对复杂任务性能和边缘部署可行性的评估。

Method: 构建包含132种系统配置的CoDyControlBench基准，涵盖自由度、系统类型、耦合水平、阻尼机制和控制器类型五个维度；评估六款主流大模型（三个商业模型：GPT、Gemini、Claude；三个开源模型：GLM、DeepSeek、Qwen），进行三次独立运行；通过推理蒸馏开发1.5B参数的轻量化模型，用于边缘部署。

Result: GPT设计成功率最高（94.8%），Qwen最低（50.0%）；自由度和控制器类型是成功率差异最大的维度；GPT与Qwen的性能差距主要源于控制器设计知识，特别是增益选择和瞬态限制机制；推理蒸馏模型在基准上优于答案蒸馏和基础模型，在1-6自由度上保持稳定，并在气动人工肌肉驱动机器人臂的三个物理试验中实现目标跟踪。

Conclusion: CoDyControlBench为评估大语言模型在复杂控制器设计任务上的性能提供了基准，验证了轻量化边缘部署模型的可行性，为大语言模型在控制工程领域的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Although remarkable capabilities have been demonstrated by Large Language Models (LLMs) across scientific domains, feedback controller design remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks focus mainly on linear single-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) systems and large API-hosted models, leaving performance on complex controller-design tasks and feasibility for edge deployment unclear. To address these limitations, we introduce the Complex Dynamics-to-Control Benchmark for Large Language Models (CoDyControlBench), comprising 132 system configurations across five evaluation dimensions: number of DoF, system type, coupling level, damping regime, and controller type. Six state-of-the-art LLMs were evaluated over three independent runs, including three commercial models (GPT, Gemini, and Claude) and three open-source models (GLM, DeepSeek, and Qwen). GPT achieved the highest design success rate at 94.8\%, whereas Qwen showed the lowest rate at 50.0\%. Across the benchmark dimensions, DoF and controller type exhibited the largest model-averaged variations in design success, with success-rate ranges of 36.3\% and 17.6\%, respectively, exceeding those associated with system type, coupling level, and damping regime. Comparison of GPT and Qwen showed that their performance gap arose mainly from the control-design knowledge, particularly gain selection and the use of transient-limiting mechanisms. For edge deployment, a specialized 1.5B-parameter model was developed through reasoning distillation. The reasoning-distilled model outperformed the answer-distilled and base model on CoDyControlBench, maintained stable performance across 1-6 DoFs, and achieved successful traget tracking in all three physical trials on a pneumatic-artificial-muscle-driven robotic arm. These results establish a benchmark baseline and highlight the potential of lightweight, edge-deployable controller-design models.

</details>


### [148] [Real-time Whole-Body Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators Carrying Arbitrarily Shaped Payloads via Kinematically-Coupled SVSDF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07005)
*Yisheng Li,Longji Yin,Tingrui Zhang,Ruize Xue,Haoda Zhu,Nan Chen,Siqi Liang,Yuxi Liu,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实时全身运动规划框架，用于移动操作机器人携带任意形状负载，通过前端碰撞检测、中间轨迹预处理和后端基于KC-SVSDF的轨迹优化，实现复杂环境中大型非凸负载的可靠运输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人在杂乱环境中运输大型非凸负载时，现有规划器要么过度简化负载几何形状，要么无法处理操作连杆间的运动耦合，导致可行空间丢失或优化停滞。

Method: 采用三段式框架：(1)前端使用链分解核碰撞检测，保持机器人和负载真实几何形状，具备紧凑存储和快速位级查询能力；(2)中间预处理阶段将前端路径转换为平滑可行连续轨迹，无碰撞时直接执行以绕过后端；(3)需要优化时使用基于运动学耦合符号有向距离场(KC-SVSDF)的轨迹优化，沿运动链传播避障梯度。

Result: 消融实验、与最先进基准的比较测试以及在差分驱动移动操作机器人上的真实世界实验均证明，所提出框架能可靠地将大型非凸负载运输通过狭窄通道和杂乱环境。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了移动操作机器人携带任意形状负载的运动规划难题，通过精确的碰撞检测、高效的路径转换和运动学感知的轨迹优化，显著提升了复杂环境下的负载运输能力。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are increasingly tasked with transporting large, non-convex payloads through cluttered environments, yet existing planners either oversimplify the payload geometry or fail to handle the kinematic coupling between manipulator links, leading to lost feasible space or stalled optimization. This letter presents a real-time whole-body motion planning framework for mobile manipulators carrying arbitrarily shaped payloads. The front-end employs a chain-decomposed kernel-based collision check that preserves the true geometry of the robot and payload, with compact storage and fast bit-level queries. A mid-end preprocessing stage converts the front-end path into a continuous trajectory enforcing smoothness and feasibility, and executes it directly when collision-free to bypass the costly back-end. When refinement is required, the back-end performs trajectory optimization built on a Kinematically-Coupled SVSDF (KC-SVSDF), which propagates collision-avoidance gradients along the kinematic chain to produce coherent whole-body escape directions. Ablation studies, comparative benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines, and real-world experiments on a differential-drive mobile manipulator demonstrate that the proposed framework reliably transports large, non-convex payloads through tight passages and cluttered environments.

</details>


### [149] [C2Dex: Contact-Consistent Reconstruction and Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07045)
*Jie Ren,Zhehao Jiang,Yinhong Yang,Haorui Jia,Han Jiang,Ben Li,Yao Yao,Cheng Lin,Qiu Shen,Zhenshan Bing,Xiao-Xiao Long,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出C2Dex框架，通过稳定物体接触点表示，将单目人体视频中的操作行为迁移到灵巧机器人，实现端到端轨迹成功率57.78%(DexYCB)和26.67%(TACO)，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量灵巧机器人操作演示数据收集成本高且困难，而单目人体视频可提供可扩展的多样化操作行为来源。然而，将此类演示迁移到灵巧机器人面临挑战：单目手物交互重建常产生时间不稳定的接触和物理不合理交互，传统重定向方法难以在不同手部实体间保持任务相关接触和局部交互几何。

Method: 构建C2Dex视频到灵巧操作框架，基于共享交互表示：通过在规范物体空间中聚合噪声帧级观测恢复稳定物体侧接触点。这些稳定接触点发挥双重作用：作为轨迹级约束引导重建时间连贯、物理合理的人体HOI轨迹；作为灵巧手的明确迁移目标，使用拉普拉斯交互优化在不同实体间保持局部手物几何，并通过残差强化学习在仿真中细化轨迹。

Result: 在DexYCB和TACO数据集上实验显示，C2Dex实现端到端轨迹成功率分别为57.78%和26.67%，在相同评估标准下显著优于最强基线方法(17.78%和10.00%)。真机回放实验进一步验证了在多样化接触密集操作任务中的物理可行性。

Conclusion: C2Dex框架通过稳定的物体接触点表示成功解决了从单目人体视频到灵巧机器人的操作行为迁移问题，在多个数据集上取得显著性能提升，并验证了真实环境下的物理可行性，为机器人学习提供了可扩展的演示数据获取方案。

Abstract: High-quality demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation are costly and difficult to collect, whereas monocular human videos provide a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors. However, transferring such demonstrations to dexterous robots remains challenging: monocular hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction often produces temporally unstable contacts and physically implausible interactions, while conventional retargeting methods struggle to preserve task-relevant contacts and local interaction geometry across different hand embodiments. We present C2Dex, a video-to-dexterous-manipulation framework built around a shared interaction representation: stable object-side contacts recovered by aggregating noisy frame-wise observations in the canonical object space. These stable contacts serve a dual role: as trajectory-level constraints that guide reconstruction toward temporally coherent and physically plausible human HOI trajectories, and as explicit transfer targets for the dexterous hand, where Laplacian interaction optimization preserves the local hand-object geometry across embodiments and residual reinforcement learning refines the trajectory in simulation. Experiments on DexYCB and TACO show that C2Dex achieves end-to-end trajectory success rates of 57.78% and 26.67%, respectively, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (17.78% and 10.00%) under identical evaluation criteria. Real-robot replay experiments further demonstrate physical feasibility across diverse contact-rich manipulation tasks. Project page: https://k-jie.github.io/C2Dex/

</details>


### [150] [AutoIntervene: Calibrated Intervention for Action-Chunking Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07065)
*Jinhe Tang,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoIntervene，一种在线框架，在部署过程中选择性地在动作分块策略和操作者之间转移控制权。该系统基于视觉-动作支持记忆评估提出的动作块，结合视觉相似性和动作一致性，自动校准切换阈值，并从成功的干预片段中提供纠正监督。在真实世界双手操作任务中的实验表明，相比手动干预，AutoIntervene提高了任务成功率并降低了操作者控制时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块视觉运动策略虽然通过预测短期动作序列而非单步命令来提高时间一致性，但仍面临感知错误和执行漂移问题。这些错误会导致机器人偏离演示分布范围，而策略继续产生与观察状态不一致的平滑动作块。需要一个系统能够在部署过程中处理这些情况，在策略失效时及时接管控制。

Method: AutoIntervene构建了一个视觉-动作支持记忆系统，基于成功任务执行建立。系统评估提出的动作块，结合视觉相似性和提出动作与参考动作之间的一致性。采用双层支持机制：局部阶段支持管理当前任务阶段内的策略到操作者转移，全局支持管理操作者恢复后返回策略控制。切换阈值从保留专家演示的评估级分数经验分位数中自动校准，避免手动调参。从成功演练中保留的干预片段用于提供纠正监督以更新策略。

Result: 在真实世界双手操作任务中的实验结果显示，与手动干预相比，AutoIntervene实现了更高的适应后任务成功率和更低的操作者控制时间。

Conclusion: AutoIntervene为动作分块视觉运动策略中的分布外情况提供了有效解决方案。通过自动阈值校准和纠正监督机制，该系统在提高任务性能的同时减少了操作者负担，实现了更智能的人机协作控制。

Abstract: Action-chunking visuomotor policies learn from demonstrations and improve temporal consistency by predicting short action sequences rather than single-step commands. Yet perception errors and execution drift can move the robot outside the demonstration distribution, while the policy continues to produce smooth action chunks that are inconsistent with the observed state. We present AutoIntervene, an online framework that selectively transfers control between an action-chunking policy and an operator during deployment. AutoIntervene evaluates proposed chunks against a visual-action support memory built from successful task executions, combining visual similarity with consistency between proposed and reference actions. Phase-local support governs policy-to-operator transfer within the current task phase, whereas global support governs the return to policy control after operator recovery. We calibrate separate switching thresholds for the two directions from empirical quantiles of evaluation-level scores on held-out expert demonstrations, avoiding direct manual tuning of score cutoffs. Intervention segments retained from successful rollouts target learner-induced states and provide corrective supervision for subsequent policy updates. Experiments on real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show higher post-adaptation task success and lower operator-control time than manual intervention. Videos and additional results are available at https://aus.bot/research/autointervene/.

</details>


### [151] [M2-SMap: Memory-Efficient Semantic Mapping with Hierarchical Multi-Model Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07074)
*QiYing Deng,ZhongLai Wang,Yuan Gao,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M2-SMap框架，通过分层多模型表示实现内存高效的语义建图，在资源受限机器人上实时运行（≥29.37 Hz），使用最少的几何原语（较最佳基线平均减少18.7%），并完全消除物体间粘连问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集点云地图内存消耗随场景规模快速增长，难以部署在资源受限机器人上；紧凑单模型表示虽降低内存成本但几何表达能力不足；现有方法多模型方法特征提取和模型选择受局部几何主导，易导致过拟合和物体粘连。

Method: M2-SMap包含四个关键步骤：(1)分层几何分解将RGB-D点云划分为紧凑高斯分量；(2)投影引导语义标注机制为每个分量分配实例身份；(3)物体感知高斯融合策略整合标注信息；(4)多尺度特征提取策略分离大面积平面区域、语义物体和复杂残余结构，分别用有界平面、物体级超二次曲面和高斯混合模型基元表示。

Result: 在三个RGB-D序列实验表明：实时运行速率不低于29.37 Hz；实现最低基元数量，较最佳基线平均减少18.7%；将每帧平均测量到的物体间粘连案例数从2.808降低至0，展示了高效且语义一致的场景表示。

Conclusion: M2-SMap通过分层多模型表示和语义感知的特征提取，有效解决了点云地图内存效率和表示灵活性的问题，为资源受限机器人提供了实时、紧凑且语义一致的建图方案。

Abstract: Dense point cloud maps, as a typically used mapping representation, are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained robots because their memory consumption grows rapidly with scene scale. Although compact single-model representations reduce memory cost, their fixed geometric expressiveness is insufficient for structurally diverse environments. Existing multi-model methods improve representational flexibility, yet their feature extraction and model selection are often dominated by local geometry, which can cause overfitting and adhesion between objects. To address these issues, this paper presents M2-SMap, a memory-efficient semantic mapping framework based on hierarchical multi-model representation. First, a hierarchical geometric decomposition partitions RGB-D point clouds into compact Gaussian components. Then, a projection-guided semantic annotation mechanism assigns instance identities to each component. Subsequently, these annotations are incorporated into an object-aware Gaussian fusion strategy. Furthermore, a multi-scale feature extraction strategy separates large planar regions, semantic objects, and complex residual structures, which are respectively represented by bounded planes, object-level superquadrics, and GMM primitives. Experiments on three RGB-D sequences show that M2-SMap runs in real time at no less than 29.37 Hz while achieving the lowest primitive count, with an average reduction of 18.7% over the best baseline. It also reduces the mean per-frame number of measured inter-object adhesion cases from 2.808 to 0, demonstrating efficient and semantically consistent scene representation.

</details>


### [152] [Detection and Ranging of Transient Extrinsic Contacts Based on 6D Dynamic Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07075)
*Haowen Zheng,Yinghao Wu,Fuyuan Liu,Yichen Li,Yitian Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TECDAR方法，利用小型6D惯性测量单元实现抓取物体的瞬态外部接触检测与测距，达到毫米级精度（平均约7毫米），响应时间180毫秒内，支持毫秒级轨迹修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类手部凭借卓越的触觉敏感性轻松定位接触，而机器人系统缺乏必要的分辨率来获取运动规划所需信息，导致操作笨拙甚至任务失败。需要一种高效方法解决抓取物体与环境间瞬时细微碰撞的感知问题。

Method: 设计夹爪尖端采用动态触觉传感，使用单个2.5×3毫米6D惯性测量单元（IMU），以7kHz采样率捕获亚毫秒级尖端变形，数据流仅84 KB/s。通过扩展卡尔曼滤波将触觉数据与机器人位姿融合，实现快速精确的外部接触定位。

Result: 系统在180毫秒内达到毫米级定位精度，实验显示线接触和点接触定位任务平均精度约7毫米。近瞬时定位使机器人能在毫秒尺度修正轨迹，实现精确工具操作和复杂环境感知。

Conclusion: 该技术通过纯触觉探索和映射推动机器人发展，在精密装配、手术辅助和触感主导环境中的自主探索等领域具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Delicate manipulation often involves transient and subtle collisions between a grasped object and the environment. While the human hand localizes these contacts effortlessly thanks to superior tactile sensitivity, robotic systems often lack the requisite resolution to acquire the information necessary for motion planning, resulting in clumsy manipulation or even task failure. Here, we propose transient extrinsic contact detection and ranging (TECDAR), a simple yet fast and efficient method for detecting and ranging extrinsic contact of grasped objects. Our design of gripper tips employs dynamic tactile sensing leveraging a single 2.5$\times$3 mm 6D inertial measurement unit. The sensor captures sub-millisecond tip deformations at a 7 kHz sampling rate, but operating on a data stream of only 84 KB/s. High bandwidth and compact data size enable the system to rapidly detect and localize contact between grasped objects and their surroundings. Specifically, fusing tactile data with robot pose via an extended Kalman filter enables fast and precise localization of extrinsic contact, reaching millimeter-level accuracy within 180 ms. Experimental results demonstrate that the system achieves an average localization accuracy of approximately 7\,mm in both line-contact and point-contact localization tasks. Furthermore, this near-instantaneous localization enables the robot to rectify its trajectory on a millisecond scale, facilitating precise tool manipulation and enhanced perception of complex environments purely through tactile exploration and mapping. We envision such techniques advancing the future of robotics across domains requiring delicate manipulation, including precision assembly, surgical assistance, and autonomous exploration in touch-dominant environments. Project page: humitlab.github.io/TECDAR/

</details>


### [153] [LifelongCrossNav: Persistent 3D Semantic Memory for Cross-Floor Multi-Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07079)
*Zehui Li,Zihao Sun,Jiawei Xu,Zheqi He,Xiaoqiang Zhang,Jing-Shu Zheng,Lu Liu,Dahui Gao,Xiuwan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LifelongCrossNav框架，通过持久化3D语义体素记忆实现多层室内环境中顺序多目标导航。该框架结合了支持感知3D可通行性建模、楼梯感知和方向感知的楼梯穿越能力，在HM3D-MFMON基准测试中显著优于传统平面持久化语义地图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象目标导航在语义感知和探索方面取得了显著进展，但多对象导航的持久记忆和跨楼层导航通常被分别处理。作者旨在解决未知多层室内环境中顺序多对象导航的持久记忆和跨楼层搜索问题，实现更高效的一体化导航框架。

Method: LifelongCrossNav框架的核心组件包括：(1)共享稀疏3D语义体素记忆，增量累积几何结构、可通行状态和视觉语言特征；(2)支持感知3D可通行性映射、楼梯特定感知和方向感知的楼梯穿越技术；(3)统一导航策略协调同层前沿探索、实时和历史兴趣点检索、楼梯导航和目标物体搜索接近；(4)基于HM3D场景构建HM3D-MFMON基准，包含需要至少一次楼层转换的子任务集合。

Result: 实验结果表明，LifelongCrossNav在HM3D-MFMON基准上持续优于代表性的平面持久化语义地图基线方法，证明了持久化3D语义记忆和跨楼层可通行性建模能有效支持多层环境中的顺序多对象导航。

Conclusion: 本文提出的LifelongCrossNav框架通过持久化3D语义记忆实现跨楼层顺序多对象导航，解决了传统方法分别处理多对象和跨楼层导航的局限性。引入的HM3D-MFMON基准为相关研究提供了新的评估平台，实验验证了所提方法的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has made substantial progress in semantic perception and exploration, yet persistent memory for multi-object navigation and cross-floor navigation are still commonly addressed separately. We present LifelongCrossNav, a framework for sequential multi-object ObjectNav in unknown multi-floor indoor environments. Within each episode, the agent receives an ordered sequence of object-goal queries while continuously maintaining a shared sparse 3D semantic voxel memory. This memory incrementally accumulates geometric structure, traversability states, and vision-language features, allowing subsequent object-goal queries to retrieve previously acquired scene information without rebuilding the map. To support persistent search across floors, LifelongCrossNav combines support-aware 3D traversability mapping, stair-specific perception, and direction-aware stair traversal. A unified navigation policy coordinates same-floor frontier exploration, live and historical point-of-interest retrieval, stair navigation, and target-object search and approach. We further introduce HM3D-MFMON, a benchmark for sequential Multi-Floor Multi-Object Navigation built on HM3D scenes, including a dedicated subset in which completing the full sequence of object-goal subtasks requires at least one floor transition. Experimental results show that LifelongCrossNav consistently outperforms a representative planar persistent semantic-map baseline on HM3D-MFMON, demonstrating that persistent 3D semantic memory and cross-floor traversability modeling effectively support sequential multi-object navigation in multi-floor environments. Project page: https://flageval-baai.github.io/LifelongCrossNavPage.

</details>


### [154] [Identifying the Key Biomechanical Features of Movement Adaptation during Exoskeleton-Assisted Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07140)
*Peter Seungjune Lee,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The understanding of natural human adaptation during exoskeleton-assisted locomotion - particularly individual differences in adaptation behaviors and temporal progression - remains limited. In this work, we investigate temporal evolution of biomechanical variables to uncover participant-specific adaptation strategies across different exoskeleton-assisted locomotion scenarios. Nine healthy participants performed treadmill walking under three conditions: without an exoskeleton, with exoskeleton active ankle assistance, and with exoskeleton zero-torque. Lower limb kinematics, inter-joint coordination, and metabolic cost of transport (MCoT) were analyzed at both the group and individual levels. Results indicate that adaptation is gradual and highly individualized, with substantial variability in convergence timing and movement patterns across participants. Kinematic adaptation occurred asynchronously across lower limb, with larger fluctuations during the swing phase. Metabolic responses were heterogeneous and often non-convergent, highlighting the limitations of steady-state assumptions commonly adopted in the literature. These findings emphasize the importance of individual-level, temporal evolution analyses for understanding adaptation dynamics in exoskeleton use.

</details>


### [155] [Representation Handoffs for OpenArm-Based Laboratory Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07154)
*Yang Shen,Chonghao Cheng,Ziyi Zhao,Jialuo Zhu,Zhenyi Yi,Qi Zhao,Jian Yang,Yuhui Shi,Chin-Teng Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个基于OpenArm的移动操作原型，用于实验室任务。通过集成双机械臂、移动底座、RGB-D感知、激光雷达映射、ROS2/MoveIt及技能接口，构建了表示交接系统，将自然语言指令转化为技能调用，将传感器观测映射为地图和物体位姿。通过干运行轨迹和启动检查评估，发现校准缺失、物体资产不全和视觉接地未完成是部署阻塞点。这些中间表示可作为语言、感知、规划和机器人安全集成的调试接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管开源机器人和基础模型降低了具身AI的门槛，但语言引导的实验室自动化仍需要从指令和观测到安全动作的可靠对齐。

Method: 构建基于OpenArm的移动操作原型，集成双OpenArm机械臂、移动底座、垂直滑台、RGB-D感知、激光雷达映射、ROS2/MoveIt执行和定义的技能接口。系统围绕表示交接组织：自然语言请求约束为已注册的技能调用，传感器观测映射为地图和物体位姿，物体先验提供角色和技能约束，运行时绑定将已验证技能编译为可执行运动目标。使用干运行轨迹和启动检查评估集成路径。

Result: 原型暴露了校准缺失、物体资产不完整、真实场景视觉接地未完成作为明确的部署阻塞点。这些中间表示作为实践调试接口。

Conclusion: 中间表示为在具身系统中集成语言、感知、规划和机器人安全提供了实用调试接口。

Abstract: Open-source robotics and foundation models have lowered the barrier to embodied AI, yet language-guided laboratory automation still requires reliable alignment from instructions and observations to safe actions. This field report presents an OpenArm-based mobile manipulation prototype for laboratory-style tasks, built by integrating dual OpenArm manipulators with a mobile base, vertical slide, RGB-D sensing, lidar-based mapping, ROS2/MoveIt execution, and profile-defined skill interfaces. The system is organized around representation handoffs: natural language requests are constrained into registered skill calls, sensor observations are grounded into maps and object poses, object priors provide role and skill constraints, and runtime bindings compile validated skills into executable motion goals. We use dry-run traces and startup checks to evaluate this integration path, showing how the prototype exposes missing calibration, incomplete object assets, and unfinished real-scene visual grounding as explicit deployment blockers. These intermediate representations serve as practical debugging interfaces for integrating language, perception, planning, and robot safety in embodied systems.

</details>


### [156] [TEMPO: Semantic-Action Decoupled RL Post-Training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07314)
*Ziheng Liu,Quantao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEMPO提出了一种针对VLA模型的语义-动作解耦双时间尺度强化学习框架，通过冻结视觉语言主干并差异化更新语义投影层（低频）和动作专家层（高频），解决了现有SFT分布失配和传统RL统一更新策略的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型适配方法存在两个核心问题：(1)监督微调(SFT)容易遭受分布失配，导致模型泛化能力受限；(2)现有强化学习(RL)方法对所有模型组件采用单一均匀更新策略，忽视了不同组件的功能角色差异，导致学习效率低下和语义表示不稳定。

Method: TEMPO框架包含三个关键设计：(1)冻结预训练视觉语言主干以保持通用语义表示的完整性；(2)将适配范围限制在两个组件上，分别为语义投影层和低层动作专家；(3)采用双时间尺度强化学习优化策略，语义投影层采用低频更新以维持潜在动作的稳定性，动作专家采用高频更新以快速整合在线交互的控制反馈。

Result: 在CALVIN基准测试和真实世界操作任务实验结果表明：(1)TEMPO持续优于预训练的最先进VLA模型和RL后训练基线方法；(2)在两个真实世界任务中达到了更高的评估奖励并保持稳定表现；(3)解耦的强化学习微调策略成功防止了快速策略更新对高级语义表示的干扰，同时允许动作专家从在线反馈中高效学习。

Conclusion: TEMPO通过语义-动作解耦的双时间尺度强化学习框架，成功解决了VLA模型在下游操作任务适配中的核心问题。该方法在保持预训练语义表示稳定性的同时，实现了动作控制的高效学习，显著提升了模型在仿真环境和真实世界操作任务中的表现，为VLA模型的强化学习后训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are commonly adapted to downstream manipulation tasks via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or online reinforcement learning (RL) post-training. SFT is prone to distribution mismatch, and existing RL approaches typically apply a single, uniform update strategy to all model components, ignoring their distinct functional roles. We propose TEMPO, a semantic-action decoupled, two-timescale RL post-training framework for VLA models. TEMPO freezes the pretrained vision-language backbone to preserve general semantic representations, and restricts adaptation to two components with dedicated RL optimization loops: the semantic projection layer and the low-level action expert. We update them at different rates--the semantic projection layer infrequently, to keep the latent action stable, and the action expert frequently, to rapidly incorporate control feedback from online interaction. This decoupling RL fine-tuning strategy prevents fast policy updates from destabilizing high-level semantic representations while still allowing the action expert to learn efficiently from online feedback. Experiments on the CALVIN benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that TEMPO consistently outperforms both pretrained state-of-the-art VLA models and the RL post-training baseline, while reaching and maintaining higher evaluation rewards on two real-world tasks.

</details>


### [157] [Learning Fault-Tolerant Locomotion with Adaptive Gait Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07328)
*Giovanbattista Gravina,Luca Rossini,Carlo Rizzardo,Arturo Laurenzi,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度强化学习的容错步态控制方法，通过非对称Actor-Critic架构和可学习步态频率参数，使四足机器人在执行器故障时仍能保持稳定性和运动能力，并在仿真和真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件故障要求足式机器人快速重组协调和步态时序以维持稳定性，尤其对于大型四足机器人，质量增加和致动器限制使得高频补偿策略难以实现，需要一种能够适应致动器功率损失的容错方法。

Method: 采用非对称Actor-Critic架构，Critic在训练时拥有特权信息，Actor从本体感知观测中学习重建潜在表示，并引入潜在对齐损失以确保Actor和Critic表示一致性；同时增强动作空间，引入可学习步态频率参数，实现自适应步态时序。

Result: 在高保真仿真中针对不平坦地形进行测试，并在68kg四足机器人上进行真实世界实验，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在致动器功率损失情况下实现容错步态控制，无需预定义故障腿策略，自适应调整步态时序，提高了四足机器人的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Abstract: Hardware failures require legged robots to rapidly reorganize coordination and gait timing to maintain stability and mobility. This is particularly challenging for larger quadrupeds, where increased mass and tighter actuation limits reduce the feasibility of aggressive, high-frequency compensation strategies often observed on smaller platforms. In this work, we propose a deep reinforcement learning approach for fault-tolerant locomotion under actuator power loss. The method employs an asymmetric actor-critic architecture in which the critic has access to privileged information during training, while the actor learns to reconstruct a corresponding latent representation from proprioceptive observations. We introduce a latent-alignment loss that encourages consistency between actor and critic representations. Additionally, we augment the action space with a learnable gait frequency parameter, enabling adaptive gait timing in response to terrain variations and actuator degradation without predefined faulty-leg strategies. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulation on uneven terrain and real-world experiments on flat ground using a 68 kg quadruped robot.

</details>


### [158] [Depth-Wise Probing and Pruning of the Planning Token in a Driving Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07361)
*Harisankar Babu,Benjamin Coors,Christopher Lang,Hendrik Berkemeyer,Tamim Asfour,Simon Foell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析驾驶VLA模型中规划token的解码过程发现：语义意图（如导航指令）在浅层已具备高可解码性（首层即达97.7%），而轨迹兼容性问题需深层逐步优化。通过移除8个非必要解码层可实现1.33×加速且仅增加约5%误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型依赖深度语言模型生成驾驶决策，但尚未明确行动阶段实际需要多少模型深度。本研究旨在量化规划token在不同解码层的语义信息密度与规划质量分布特征。

Method: 采用轨迹空间logit lens技术，对32层解码器逐层提取规划token并评估两项指标：(1) 导航指令的线性可解码性（通过探针准确率测量）；(2) 与固定原生规划器的轨迹兼容性（以闭环Avg-L2距离量化）。同时测试早期层级的读出网络有效性，并评估解码层精简策略。

Result: 语义意图在首层即达到97.7%指令探针准确率（对比16.7%随机基线），但轨迹兼容性随层级加深持续改善，最终层Avg-L2误差降至2.11米。首层读出网络可部分弥合此差距。移除8个引起最大角度偏转的层级后，误差增幅控制在5%内，解码速度提升33%。未发现特定场景退化现象。

Conclusion: 驾驶VLA模型的核心规划信息集中于浅层，深层主要进行格式转换而非信息生成。该发现支持轻量化设计可能性，但结论受限于ORION检查点和Bench2Drive测试环境。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models route driving decisions through a deep language model, but it is unclear how much of that depth the action itself requires. We study a representative driving VLA whose entire plan is carried by a single planning token that a generative planner decodes into a trajectory. Borrowing the planner as a trajectory-space logit lens, we decode the planning token from every one of the 32 decoder layers and measure two signals: the linear decodability of the navigation command and trajectory compatibility with the frozen native planner. Our diagnostic shows that semantic intent is linearly decodable early: command-probe accuracy reaches 97.7\% after the first decoder layer, compared with 16.7\% chance. In contrast, compatibility with the frozen native planner improves gradually across depth, with open-loop Avg-L2 reaching its minimum of 2.11\,m only at the final layer. Learned readouts from the first layer recover much of this gap, indicating that planning information is already present early but is not yet represented in the format expected by the deployed planner. Ranking decoder layers by the angular deviation they induce in the planning token permits removal of 8 of 32 layers within an approximately 5\% relative open-loop error increase and yields a measured 1.33$\times$ decoder speedup. At the evaluated sample size, no family-specific degradation is statistically resolved. These findings are limited to the evaluated ORION checkpoint and Bench2Drive setup.

</details>


### [159] [TRACE: Ergodic Trajectory Optimization for Active Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02304)
*Ziyue Zheng,Linli Shi,Bingkun He,Wen Jiang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于遍历覆盖的主动重建方法：通过在线估计场景信息分布并规划与之统计匹配的传感器轨迹，克服了传统贪心NBV方法的低效性，在Replica数据集上PSNR提升1.5 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅（Gaussian-splatting）的主动重建系统采用贪心式单步NBV选择与短视野路径规划，忽略了场景信息的全局结构，导致在视点间转移时浪费传感能力，产生低效轨迹。

Method: 本文提出将主动重建建模为遍历覆盖问题：在线根据当前地图的不确定性与可见性生成目标信息分布，并利用核遍历水平规划器（基于梯度流和足迹耗尽机制）计算与之匹配的遍历轨迹，从而闭合映射与轨迹优化的闭环。

Result: 在Replica数据集上，该方法相比NBV基线将PSNR提升了1.5 dB，验证了遍历覆盖策略在提升重建质量方面的优势。

Conclusion: 通过提出基于遍历覆盖的主动重建框架，本文实现了比贪心式NBV方法更高的重建质量，证明了全局信息分布与轨迹统计特性对齐的有效性。

Abstract: Existing active reconstruction systems with Gaussian-splatting maps select observations greedily, optimizing a single next-best-view (NBV) at each step and connecting the chosen views by short-horizon path planning. This greedy decoupling disregards the global structure of scene information, producing inefficient trajectories that waste sensing capacity in transit between selected views. In this work, we study active reconstruction as an ergodic coverage problem: the time-averaged spatial statistics of the sensor trajectory should match a target information distribution induced by the current map. Our approach derives this target distribution online from uncertainty and visibility, and calculates ergodic trajectories via a kernel-ergodic horizon planner with gradient flow and footprint depletion, closing the loop between mapping and trajectory optimization. We thoroughly evaluate TRACE on the Replica dataset against the Next-Best-View (NBV) baselines, improving PSNR by 1.5 dB. Code: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/trace-active-reconstruction.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Self-Healing 6G Networks-in-Network for Resilient Wireless Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06423)
*Daniel Lindenschmitt,Anton Schösser,Afnan Aghai,Philipp Schulz,Gerhard Fettweis,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自愈合网络中网络(NiN)架构，通过将频谱异常检测与动态频谱管理集成在单一控制回路中，实现6G网络的快速自主适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来6G网络需要在高度动态的无线环境中管理多个自主子网络(SN)共享频谱资源，故障设备或故意干扰可能破坏正在进行的通信，因此需要快速自主的网络适应能力。

Method: 提出自愈合NiN架构，频谱扫描器持续监测频谱并将检测到的异常转发给动态频谱管理(DSM)系统，DSM自动识别合适的频率资源并重新配置受影响的子网络。

Result: 现场演示中，参与者可以发起受控干扰并实时观察整个适应过程，从异常检测到自主频率重新分配和网络恢复。

Conclusion: 将频谱监测和资源管理集成到单一控制回路中可以提升未来NiN实现的弹性，展示了自主频谱感知网络的实际方法。

Abstract: Future 6G networks must manage increasingly dynamic radio environments in which multiple autonomous sub-networks (SNs) share frequency resources and adapt to changing operating conditions. In such scenarios, interference from faulty devices or intentional jamming can disrupt ongoing communications, making rapid and autonomous network adaptation essential. This demonstration presents a self-healing networks-in-network (NiN) architecture that closely integrates the detection of spectrum anomalies with dynamic spectrum management. A spectrum scanner continuously monitors the frequency spectrum and forwards detected anomalies to the DSM, which automatically identifies suitable frequency resources and reconfigures the affected SN. During the live demonstration, participants can initiate controlled disruptions and observe the entire adaptation process in real time, from anomaly detection to autonomous frequency reallocation and network recovery. The demonstrator illustrates how integrating spectrum monitoring and resource management into a single control loop can improve the resilience of future NiN implementations and demonstrates a practical approach to autonomous, spectrum-aware networking.

</details>


### [161] [FedTransKD-IDS: Robust Federated Transfer Learning with Knowledge Distillation for Intrusion Detection in IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06447)
*Mohammad Hosssein Gholamrezazadeh,Ahmadreza MontazerolghaemAhmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对物联网、5G分布式场景下联邦学习入侵检测系统受统计异质性影响、效率稳定性差的痛点，本研究提出FedTransKD-IDS框架，融合鲁棒聚合、联邦迁移学习与知识蒸馏技术，实现高隐私保护下的高效检测，性能达到最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式网络（物联网、5G环境）的高隐私保护与可扩展性要求给入侵检测系统带来显著挑战：现有联邦学习虽可通过避免数据集中化实现隐私保护，但在边缘节点统计异质性严重、资源受限的场景下，其效率与稳定性大幅下滑，亟需针对性优化方案。

Method: 提出FedTransKD-IDS框架，整合基于几何均值的鲁棒聚合、联邦迁移学习、知识蒸馏三项技术：协作训练得到的全局教师模型将其特征提取组件迁移至轻量级学生模型，兼顾部署效率与性能表现。

Result: 在异构数据集上的实验验证显示，该框架检测准确率高达99.18%，召回率达99.99%，证实结构化知识迁移方案在联邦环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了联邦环境入侵检测系统兼顾隐私、效率与稳定性的问题，为资源受限、数据异构的边缘分布式网络场景下的入侵检测提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In modern distributed network environments, particularly in Internet of Things infrastructures and 5G networks, stringent privacy preservation and scalability requirements have created significant challenges for intrusion detection systems. Although federated learning preserves privacy by preventing data centralization, its efficiency and stability is considerably degraded under severe statistical heterogeneity and resource constraints of edge nodes. To address these limitations, this study introduces the FedTransKD-IDS framework, which enhances both system stability and efficiency by integrating robust aggregation based on the geometric mean, federated transfer learning, and knowledge distillation. Within this framework, the collaboratively trained global teacher model transfers its feature extraction component to lightweight student models. Experimental evaluation on heterogeneous datasets demonstrates a peak detection performance, achieving an accuracy of 99. 18% and a recall of 99. 99%, thereby indicating the effectiveness of structured knowledge transfer in federated environments.

</details>


### [162] [Detecting and Characterizing Massively Shared IP Addresses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06517)
*Amanda Hsu,Paul Pearce,Frank Li,Arthur Berger,Philipp Richter*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究利用大型CDN数据，通过分析流量日变化模式检测并刻画了“大规模共享”IP地址，发现其在IPv4流量中占比显著，地域分布不均且随时间增长，而IPv6中此类现象较少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IP地址因NAT和代理等技术被广泛共享，大规模共享对IP归因、黑名单、速率限制等机制产生重大影响，处理不当会影响大量终端用户与组织。

Method: 基于大型CDN的全局数据，利用流量形状的日变化模式来检测并表征大规模共享IP，并结合网络特征（蜂窝连接、双栈能力等）进行上下文分析。

Result: 大规模共享IP占IPv4流量比例较高，超过40%的流量来自不到2%的活跃IP；地理分布上，部分较小国家共享率较高；IPv6中此类地址较少，但部分移动运营商例外；识别了云网络中代理服务的多个大规模共享IP；趋势显示此类流量随时间持续增长。

Conclusion: 研究为IP共享现状提供了全局视角，揭示了大规模共享IP的广泛存在及其增长趋势，表明未来将更加依赖这些共享技术。

Abstract: IP addresses are commonly shared across devices and users for a variety of reasons, including NAT and proxies. These technologies operate at different scales, from residential NATs that share an IP address across devices in a home to large-scale Carrier Grade NATs that share hundreds or thousands of users on a single IP. Cases of large-scale IP sharing are distinct as they have significant implications for IP-based mechanisms such as attribution, blocklisting, and rate-limiting, where the consequences of mishandling affect a large quantity of end-users and organizations.
  In this work, we detect and characterize IP addresses shared at large scales, which we coin massively shared. Leveraging diurnal patterns in traffic shape, we use data from a large CDN to characterize these IPs globally. We broadly find that massive IP sharing is responsible for a large fraction of IPv4 traffic, concentrated in a small fraction of address space, with over 40% of total traffic coming from less than 2% of active IP addresses. We observe distinct patterns in deployment geographically, with particularly high rates of massively shared traffic from some smaller countries. Comparatively, in IPv6, we find far fewer massively shared addresses with some surprising exceptions among mobile providers. We additionally contextualize these addresses by other network characteristics, including identifying cellular connectivity and dual-stack capabilities, and identifying several instances of massively shared IPs in proxy services hosted on cloud networks. Finally, we find that rates of massively shared traffic are increasing over time, predicting future reliance on these technologies. Our work contextualizes the state of IP sharing, providing a uniquely broad perspective globally.

</details>


### [163] [Improving the Energy Efficiency of High Throughput Computing: A Measurement-Based Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06622)
*Damu Ding,Xinpeng Hong,Alastair Dewhurst,James Walder,Daniel Schien,David Greenwood,Noa Zilberman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 一项基于测量的数据中心高吞吐计算案例研究，分析实际数据中心功耗数据，研究服务器功耗优化技术，提出兼顾性能与碳排放的节能建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心能耗巨大，不仅带来成本压力，还产生大量碳排放。服务器能效是数据中心运营商的关键考量，需要理解不同运行条件下的服务器功耗特征，以实现节能目标。

Method: 采用基于测量的案例研究方法，分析运营中的数据中心的功耗使用信息，结合针对代表性高吞吐工作负载的功耗降低技术的定向测量，评估服务器配置调整对作业处理能耗的影响。

Result: 揭示了数据中心运营商在最小化能耗和碳排放过程中遇到的障碍，讨论了服务器配置调整对处理作业能耗的影响，识别出影响能效的关键因素。

Conclusion: 提出了可操作的服务器能耗降低建议，在保证性能的同时兼顾碳排放问题，为数据中心能效优化提供实践指导。

Abstract: The significant energy consumed by data centers has become a concern both for costs and associated carbon emissions. In particular, the energy efficiency of servers is a key consideration for data center operators, and understanding servers' power consumption under different operating conditions is an important aspect of it. In this paper, we present a measurement-based case study of high-throughput computing. We analyze power usage information of an operational data center, combined with focused measurements of power reduction techniques for a representative high-throughput workload. The study points out the obstacles encountered by data center operators in their efforts to minimize energy consumption and carbon emissions, and discusses the impact of server configuration adjustments on the energy consumption of processing jobs. We offer actionable recommendations for decreasing the energy usage of servers, while considering both performance and carbon emissions.

</details>


### [164] [A Parameter-Specific Retrieval and Knowledge-Guided Reasoning Framework for LLM-Based GPSR Optimization in FANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06760)
*Zhipeng Lin,Bin Duo,Tong Liu,Jie Lin,Jianting Yuan,Xiaojun Yuan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PMKR-GPSR框架，一种基于大语言模型(GPSR)的自适应参数优化方法，用于飞阅自组织网络(FANETs)中的无状态路由协议，通过参数特定多索引检索和知识引导推理机制，显著提升了高动态环境下的网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GPSR的FANETs路由协议难以在高度动态环境中自适应调整路由参数（如hello间隔、多路径数量、贪婪转发权重）。大语言模型(LLMs)作为新兴AI技术展现出智能决策潜力，为GPSR参数的自适应调整提供了新机遇。然而，将LLMs应用于GPSR仍面临挑战：不相关经验检索和协议约束缺失问题。

Method: 本文提出PMKR-GPSR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)参数特定多索引检索机制，为LLMs提供参数相关经验，同时减少无关信息干扰；(2)知识引导约束图，强制路由参数满足依赖规则和优化约束，确保协议一致性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，PMKR-GPSR在高移动性FANETs环境下实现了更高的数据包投递率和更低的端到端延迟，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: PMKR-GPSR框架成功实现了协议一致的路由参数自适应调整，克服了传统GPSR协议在动态环境中的局限性，以及LLM直接应用的挑战。该方法通过结构化知识引导和精准经验检索，为AI赋能的网络路由优化提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Existing Greedy Perimeter Stateless Routing (GPSR)-based protocols for Flying Ad-Hoc Networks (FANETs) struggle to adapt routing parameters, such as hello interval, multi-path number, and greedy forwarding weights, under highly dynamic environments. As an emerging artificial intelligence technology, large language models (LLMs) show potential for intelligent decision-making, providing new opportunities for adaptive adjustment of GPSR parameters to improve network performance. However, applying LLMs to GPSR remains challenging due to irrelevant experience retrieval and the absence of protocol constraints. To address these issues, we propose a Parameter-Specific Multi-Index Retrieval and Knowledge-Guided Reasoning framework for adaptive GPSR optimization (PMKR-GPSR), an LLM-based framework that enables protocol-consistent routing parameter adaptation. We design a parameter-specific multi-index retrieval mechanism to provide LLMs with parameter-relevant experiences while reducing interference from irrelevant information. We further construct a knowledge-guided constraint graph to enforce that the routing parameters satisfy dependency rules and optimization constraints. Simulation results demonstrate that PMKR-GPSR achieves higher packet delivery ratio and lower end-to-end delay under high-mobility FANETs.

</details>


### [165] [EvoRIC: Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuned LLM-empowered RAN Intelligent Control Toward Autonomous O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06789)
*Lingyan Bao,Jemin Lee,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoRIC框架，通过分层架构结合非实时RIC和近实时RIC，利用强化学习微调机制使大语言模型具备网络域特定决策能力，实现在集成接入和回传网络中的自主演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI技术在无线接入网络(RAN)中取得了进展，但存在关键挑战：传统机器学习算法在不同网络拓扑下的泛化能力有限，而通用大语言模型(LLM)计算需求高且缺乏领域特定知识。需要一种既能利用LLM能力又具备网络领域知识的方法。

Method: 提出EvoRIC分层架构框架，利用非实时RIC进行全局模型更新，近实时RIC进行本地执行。采用基于强化学习的微调(RLFT)机制，LLM作为近端策略优化(PPO)智能体的actor，通过无线环境交互元组迭代更新LLM参数，使语义推理与网络性能目标对齐。

Result: 在集成接入和回传(IAB)网络中评估了EvoRIC框架的泛化能力和有效性，证明了该方法在动态网络环境中的可行性。

Conclusion: 讨论了EvoRIC框架面临的开放挑战和未来方向，为实现自主O-RAN提供了技术路径和研究基础。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in applying artificial intelligence (AI) techniques to radio access network (RAN), critical challenges remain: traditional machine learning (ML) algorithms suffer from limited generalization across varying network topologies, whereas general-purpose large language models (LLMs) face high computational demands and lack domain-specific knowledge. To address these gaps, this article introduces the evolving RAN intelligent controller (RIC) (EvoRIC) framework, a hierarchical architecture that enables continuous evolution by leveraging a non-real-time RIC (non-RT RIC) for global model updates and a near-real-time RIC (near-RT RIC) for local execution, dynamically empowering LLMs with domain-specific decision-making capabilities. Within this framework, we employ a reinforcement learning-based fine-tuning (RLFT) mechanism where an LLM operates as an actor within a proximal policy optimization (PPO) agent. By leveraging the interaction tuples collected from the wireless environment, the LLM's parameters are iteratively updated to align semantic reasoning with rigorous network performance objectives. We evaluate the generalization and efficacy of the proposed EvoRIC framework within integrated access and backhaul (IAB) networks, and finally, discuss the open challenges and future directions of the EvoRIC framework toward realizing autonomous O-RAN.

</details>


### [166] [LYRA: Label-Free Structural Synchronization and Resource Allocation for UAV Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07392)
*Feng He,Alireza Furutanpey,Paolo Bellavista,Yu Qiu,Jiangchuan Liu,Jiannong Cao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向UAV边缘智能系统的联合模型更新调度与资源分配框架，通过在线语义分歧率(OSDR)作为更新触发代理，实现细粒度敏感度感知结构同步(SASS)，并采用Lyapunov引导的离散强化学习算法进行动作空间降维和约束处理，以最大化长期语义保真度和资源效率；实验表明该框架在语义恢复效率和更新触发精度上均优于基线方法，并满足长期能源预算，将动态环境腐蚀场景下的平均风险积压降低33.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV边缘系统在处理关键任务时，需在环境干扰下自适应更新层次化视觉模型以维持推理可靠性。现有研究忽略了模型更新的最佳时机、实时专家标签的不切实际性，以及UAV的带宽和能源约束问题。

Method: 提出联合模型更新调度与资源分配框架。将在线语义分歧率(OSDR)作为无标签语义评估的代理，用于及时更新触发，实现敏感度感知结构同步(SASS)。采用Lyapunov引导的离散强化学习算法，通过动作空间降维和将约束转化为虚拟队列稳定性问题，解决混合动作空间的维度灾难并有效控制长期能源预算。

Result: 基于真实交通轨迹的实验结果表明，所提框架在语义恢复效率和更新触发精度方面持续优于代表性基线，满足长期能源预算，并将动态环境腐蚀场景下的平均风险积压最多降低33.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过无标签评估、降维和能源约束处理，实现了UAV边缘智能系统的高长期语义保真度和资源效率，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: While deploying hierarchical vision models to process mission-critical tasks, UAV edge systems must adaptively update the models to sustain inference reliability under low-level environmental corruption. However, existing work has overlooked the optimal timing for model updates, the impracticality of relying on real-time expert labels, and the significant bandwidth and energy constraints of UAVs. This paper proposes a joint model update scheduling and resource allocation framework, aiming to maximize long-term semantic fidelity and resource efficiency of UAV edge intelligence systems. To address the challenge of label-free semantic evaluation, we formulate the Online Semantic Disagreement Rate (OSDR) as a proxy for timely update triggering, thereby enabling fine-grained Sensitivity-Aware Structural Synchronization (SASS). Furthermore, to overcome the curse of dimensionality in hybrid action spaces and effectively bound long-term energy budgets, we propose a Lyapunov-guided discrete reinforcement learning algorithm that performs action space dimensionality reduction and transforms constraints into virtual queue stability problems. The reported experimental results, based on real traffic traces, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative baselines in semantic recovery efficiency and update triggering precision, by satisfying long-term energy budget and by reducing average risk backlog by up to 33.3\% in the dynamic environmental corruption scenario.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了MSB-GFM，一种针对跨域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架。通过多语义基表示学习和域对抗双通道架构，解决了多标签语义纠缠及跨域泛化差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多标签节点分类方法主要局限于域内场景，跨域泛化能力不足。虽然图基础模型（GFMs）展现出跨域学习的潜力，但它们通常基于单标签假设，导致多标签节点在表示空间中产生语义纠缠，难以同时准确区分多个标签。

Method: 提出了多语义基图基础模型（MSB-GFM）。主要包含两个核心创新：1. 引入多语义基表示学习范式，将多标签节点建模为多个语义基的自适应组合，以灵活捕捉多重语义；2. 开发具有域对抗训练的语义-结构双通道架构，促进有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 广泛的实验结果证明了该模型在跨域多标签节点分类任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM为跨域多标签节点分类提供了一个有效的解决方案，克服了传统图基础模型基于单标签假设导致的语义纠缠问题，显著提升了模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [168] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 字节级大模型常将字节分组为动态patch，但传统架构仍对所有patch使用相同的稠密计算。EntropyMoE引入MoE架构，以patch entropy为路由信号选择Top-K专家，实现更高效的稀疏计算，在不降低下游性能的前提下大幅降低bits-per-byte，扩展了MoE在字节级建模中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字节级patch模型对每个patch使用相同的稠密前馈计算，无法根据patch的语义和粒度变化自适应地分配模型容量，导致计算效率与表达能力受限。

Method: 用Top-K专家层替换全局patch Transformer中的稠密前馈模块；以动态patch为专家路由的基本单元，并根据其字节覆盖量确定工作负载分配；路由器直接根据patch entropy选择专家，利用与动态patch构建相同的粒度信号组织稀疏计算；patch entropy与长度共同构成调节专家专业化的特征空间。

Result: 实验表明EntropyMoE在匹配的稠密与稀疏基线中取得最低的held-out bits-per-byte，同时下游任务准确率保持相当水平。

Conclusion: 该论文提出EntropyMoE，将MoE架构与动态字节patch结合，实现了比稠密基线更低的bits-per-byte，同时保持下游任务性能。这表明patch entropy可作为稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，将MoE建模从基于分词器的表示扩展到字节级动态patch。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [170] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method.
