### [237] [SetEasy: A Multi-Modal Classroom Engagement Assessment and Seating Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07188)
*Zhihao Xie,Hongye Yang,Shien Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SetEasy系统通过多模态感知数据和优化算法，在固定座位布局中优化课堂座位安排，显著提升学生参与度，平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，无需硬件改造即可实现数据驱动的座位策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对全球同质化背景下，需要创造文化响应性、差异化空间设计的需求；旨在固定座位网格中优化课堂参与度，通过数据驱动策略在不改变硬件的前提下提升学生参与效果。

Method: 融合多模态感知（腕带生理数据、4K视频、环境数据），基于修订版ISEQ训练v-Gage模型；每周将两周参与度预测映射至学生-座位效用矩阵，采用CP-SAT优化算法在视觉可及性和社交动态约束下生成座位方案。

Result: 在为期四周的部署中（23名学生、331节课），v-Gage在情感、行为、认知及整体维度均收敛，RMSE从0.75降至0.53；优化后平均参与度从0.30提升至0.70，超过三分之二的座位达到高参与度，后排低活动模式显著减少。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的座位策略可大幅提升参与度而无需硬件改变；"评估+优化"范式为文化响应性、差异化空间设计提供了可转移、可持续的路径，在全球同质化背景下具有广泛应用价值。

Abstract: SetEasy optimizes classroom engagement in fixed seating grids. It fuses multimodal sensing (wristband physiology, 4K video, environmental data) and trains a v-Gage model grounded in a revised ISEQ. Each week, two-week engagement forecasts are mapped to a student-seat utility matrix, and CP-SAT generates seating plans under visual-access and social-dynamics constraints. In a four-week deployment (23 students, 331 classes), v-Gage converged across affective, behavioral, cognitive, and overall dimensions, cutting RMSE from 0.75 to 0.53. Optimization raised mean engagement from 0.30 to 0.70, with over two-thirds of seats reaching high engagement and back-row low-activity patterns markedly reduced. These results show that, without hardware changes, interpretable, data-driven seating strategies can substantially enhance engagement. The multimodal "assessment + optimization" paradigm offers a transferable, sustainable path to culturally responsive, differentiated spatial design amid global homogenization.

</details>


### [238] [EMAS: Stabilizing Multi-Agent System Evolution through Evidence-Guided Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07196)
*Chao Fei,Qingyi Si,Kaihua Liang,Yanghua Xiao,Panos Kalnis,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EMAS框架，通过利用样本经验动态修订多智能体系统的拓扑和提示词，在不更新LLM参数的前提下，显著提升了系统准确性并降低了token成本。实验结果表明，EMAS能有效将新样本经验转化为可重用的系统更新，在多个基准测试中均展现出优越的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化多智能体系统设计方法通常在初始设计阶段优化提示词和拓扑结构，随后在后续样本上部署不变的系統。样本经验很少被整合为可重用的系统更新，且面向准确性的设计可能导致高昂的token成本。

Method: 提出EMAS（Evolving Multi-Agent System）框架，通过记录样本运行轨迹生成结构化诊断信息，确定修订操作和目标；仅在相同诊断在多个样本中重复出现时生成候选修订方案，并通过配对验证确保修订满足接受标准后才应用，整个过程不涉及LLM参数更新。

Result: 在四个基准测试和两个大语言模型上，EMAS获得了最高的任务加权整体准确性，在8个模型-基准设置中的6个达到最优或并列最优。在两个演化周期内，EMAS在Kimi-K2-6和Qwen3.6-27B上分别实现了6.30%和20.10%的任务加权准确性相对提升。在MBPP基准测试中使用Qwen3.6-27B时，EMAS将准确性从55.09%提升至89.12%，同时将每个任务的token使用量减少了62.2%。

Conclusion: EMAS成功将新样本中的经验转化为多智能体系统拓扑和提示词的可重用更新，实现了在提升准确性的同时降低成本的目标。

Abstract: Many methods for automated multi-agent system design optimize prompts and topologies during an initial design stage and then deploy the resulting system unchanged on subsequent samples. Experience from these samples is rarely consolidated into reusable system updates, while accuracy-oriented designs may incur high token costs. We introduce EMAS (Evolving Multi-Agent System), which uses this experience to revise MAS topology and prompts without updating LLM parameters, either to improve accuracy or to reduce cost. EMAS converts traces into structured diagnoses that specify a revision operation and target. It generates a candidate revision only when the same diagnosis recurs across samples and applies it only if paired validation against the current MAS meets the corresponding acceptance criterion. Across four benchmarks and two LLMs, EMAS attains the highest task-weighted overall accuracy for both backbones and is best or tied in six of eight model--benchmark settings. Within two evolution epochs, EMAS achieves relative gains of 6.30% and 20.10% in task-weighted accuracy on Kimi-K2-6 and Qwen3.6-27B, respectively. On MBPP with Qwen3.6-27B, EMAS raises accuracy from 55.09% to 89.12% while reducing token use per task by 62.2%. These results show that EMAS can turn experience from new samples into reusable updates to MAS topology and prompts.

</details>


### [239] [Authoring and Management of Transparent Research Integrity Assessments of Randomised Clinical Trial Publications Using LLM-assisted Tools and Provenance Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07202)
*Milan Markovic,Goutham Indukuri,Somayajulu Sripada,Colby J. Vorland,Jack Wilkinson,Clare Robertson,Mark Bolland,Andrew Grey,Miriam Brazzelli,Alison Avenell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍INSPECT-AI，一种基于大语言模型的交互式工具，可辅助研究人员根据社区认可的INSPECT-SR框架和RIPE-O本体，对已发表的随机对照试验进行科研诚信评估，并构建了包含140项专家评估的科研诚信来源与证据知识图谱（RIPE-KG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验的系统评价是临床指南的重要依据，必须满足高科研诚信标准；然而，人工评估过程复杂、耗时且主观性强，存在分歧风险，亟需高效、一致的辅助工具。

Method: 开发基于大语言模型的INSPECT-AI工具，整合INSPECT-SR框架与RIPE-O本体，实现交互式评估；生成包含95篇RCT文献、140项专家评估的RIPE-KG知识图谱。

Result: 成功构建INSPECT-AI工具及RIPE-KG知识图谱，展示了工具自动生成科研诚信评估的能力，并通过本体记录评估过程来源。

Conclusion: INSPECT-AI为科研诚信评估提供了创新方案，提升评估效率与一致性，同时保障高质量标准，有望改善临床证据可靠性。

Abstract: Systematic reviews of Randomised Controlled Trials (RCTs) are routinely used as evidence for clinical care guidelines. Such evidence has to meet high research integrity standards to prevent low quality or false research outputs influencing the clinical care. However, assessing research integrity of published RCTs is a complex process requiring manual effort, and potentially resulting in diverse opinions of the human assessors. This paper describes INSPECT-AI, an LLM-based interactive tool that assists human reviewers with research integrity assessments of published RCTs based on the community approved INSPECT-SR framework, and the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence ontology (RIPE-O) for documenting the provenance of the assessment process. In addition, we present the Research Integrity Provenance and Evidence knowledge graph (RIPE-KG), an initial set of 140 expert research integrity assessments of 95 RCT publications generated by INSPECT-AI and described using RIPE-O.

</details>


### [240] [Beyond the Black Box: Interpretable Models of Human Randomisation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07220)
*Ngoc Linh Dao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在O'Neill零和卡片游戏中，人类玩家偏离混合策略均衡的重复/避免行为是否可通过透明可解释模型（而非黑箱模型如LSTM）来预测，并揭示其背后的行为结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合策略均衡预测独立同分布（i.i.d.）行为，即过去行动不应帮助预测未来决策。然而，人类玩家系统性偏离这一基准，且黑箱序列模型（如LSTM）可预测这些偏离。本文旨在探讨这种预测能力是否可通过透明可解释模型实现，同时揭示其行为结构。

Method: 基于2,802对玩家的84,060次决策数据，首先将朴素模型和行为模型与可解释机器学习模型及深度学习模型进行基准对比；然后评估先前工作的改进版EWA（经验加权吸引）规范，并使用LASSO诊断方法论证嵌套频率追踪扩展的合理性。

Result: 结果显示，重复或避免行为，尤其是玩家对自身近期行动历史的管理，解释了大部分可解释且战略上可利用的信号；而频率追踪在样本外预测中贡献甚微。

Conclusion: 黑箱模型（如LSTM）的预测能力可通过聚焦于重复/避免行为的透明可解释模型大部分实现，频率追踪扩展的额外收益有限。

Abstract: Mixed strategy equilibrium predicts i.i.d play: past actions should not help predict future decisions. Human players, however, systematically depart from this benchmark, and in O'Neill's zero sum card game, these departures can be predicted by black box sequence models such as LSTMs. This paper asks whether that predictive power can be achieved by transparent alternatives that also reveal the behavioural structure behind it. Using 84,060 decisions from 2,802 pairs, the analysis first benchmarks naive and behavioral models against interpretable machine learning and deep learning models, then evaluates the modified EWA specifications of prior work against these benchmarks and uses the LASSO diagnostics to motivate a further nested frequency tracking extension. The results show that repeat or avoid behavior, especially players' management of their own recent action histories, accounts for most of the interpretable and strategically exploitable signal, while frequency tracking adds little out of sample.

</details>


### [241] [From probability to causality in probabilistic logic programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07230)
*Zora Wurm,Kilian Rückschloß,Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究概率逻辑程序中因果序的歧义性问题，利用其与贝叶斯网络的关系推导唯一确定因果序的条件，并结合关系结构约束，提出了验证学习到的概率逻辑程序是否具有明确干预语义的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当概率逻辑程序的结构从数据中学习时，仅使用概率信息，单一概率分布可能与多种因果序兼容，导致干预推理中的歧义性。因此需要探究在何种情况下因果序能被分布唯一确定。

Method: 利用无环概率逻辑程序与贝叶斯网络之间的关系，推导概率信息唯一确定因果序的条件；同时考虑由底层关系词汇诱导的因果对称性集合，将关系结构产生的约束纳入考量。

Result: 提出了一种验证方法，用于判断学习到的概率逻辑程序是否支持明确定义的干预语义。该方法能够识别在何种条件下概率逻辑程序中的因果序是唯一的。

Conclusion: 本研究为解决概率逻辑程序中的因果推理歧义问题提供了理论框架和实用方法，对于构建具有明确干预能力的统计关系人工智能系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Probabilistic logic programming is a formalism of statistical relational artificial intelligence that supports causal queries, including interventions from outside the system. When the structure of a probabilistic logic program is learned from data, however, only probabilistic information is used, and a single probability distribution may be compatible with several causal orders. This leads to ambiguity in interventional reasoning, raising the question of when the causal order is uniquely determined by the distribution. Exploiting the relationship between acyclic probabilistic logic programs and Bayesian networks, we derive conditions under which the probabilistic information encoded in a program determines a unique causal order. We also incorporate constraints arising from relational structure by taking into account prescribed sets of causal symmetries induced by the underlying relational vocabulary. The result is a method for verifying when a learned probabilistic logic program supports well-defined intervention semantics.

</details>


### [242] [Winning by Peeking: Unenforced Budgets and Test-Set Selection Inflate Short-Budget AutoML Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07303)
*Guilin Zhang,Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 短时AutoML系统比较极易受评估协议缺陷误导。案例显示Orcetra看似优于FLAML和AutoGluon，实则源于测试集过拟合和预算执行不严。修正协议后，其性能优势消失，提示此类比较需谨慎处理验证机制和计算资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对短时预算（几十秒）下AutoML系统比较中普遍存在且容易出错的评估问题，旨在揭示导致错误比较结果的协议缺陷。

Method: 在513个OpenML数据集上对比Orcetra、FLAML和AutoGluon。通过修正实验协议（将模型选择移至验证集、外部强制执行时间预算、确保各框架计算资源均等）来重跑实验，并量化误差来源。

Result: 原始协议下Orcetra胜率显著（60秒预算下胜57.1%，30秒预算下对FLAML胜78.4%）。修正协议后，Orcetra胜率降至34.3%，与其他系统的性能差异不再显著。误差主要归因于测试集过拟合（选择偏差）和不均等的实际计算时间。

Conclusion: 短预算比较中协议细节（如验证集划分、计算资源分配）对结果有决定性影响。作者提出了短预算AutoML比较的检查清单，并公开了代码和复现脚本以确保研究透明度。

Abstract: Comparisons between AutoML systems at short time budgets -- tens of seconds rather than hours -- are common in tool READMEs and workshop papers, and they are easy to get wrong. We report a case study in which a simple AutoML engine, Orcetra, appeared to beat FLAML and AutoGluon on 513 OpenML datasets, winning 57.1% of them at a nominal 60-second budget and 78.4% of datasets against FLAML alone at 30 seconds. Both margins came from protocol defects that a results table cannot show. The search loop scored every candidate on the test split and reported the best, making the headline metric a maximum over dozens of noisy estimates while the baselines selected on training data and touched the test set once; and the budget was checked before launching a candidate but never enforced during one, so the system consumed a median of 120 s against a 60-second budget, 2.24x the wall-clock AutoGluon used. Re-running with selection moved to a validation split, the deadline enforced externally and every framework pinned to an equal share of the machine, Orcetra's win rate on the re-run subset falls from 59.4% to 34.3% and no pairwise difference against either competitor remains significant. Recording both estimands inside a single search lets us attribute the collapse: the selection rule accounts for 4.8 percentage points and unequal compute for most of the rest. The same traces give the selection bias as a function of budget, measured rather than assumed: it grows with $K$ but reaches only 0.27 accuracy points, about five times below the $σ\sqrt{2\ln K}$ bound a marginal-standard-error argument predicts, because candidates scored on shared test rows cancel most of the noise. We close with a checklist for short-budget comparisons. Code, per-dataset results and the scripts that regenerate every number and figure in the paper are released with it.

</details>


### [243] [An End-to-End Agent Auditing Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07346)
*Haoning Wang,Mingxun Zhang,Chenyue Yu,Yingjun Shang,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A²E（Agent Auditing Engine），一个用于评估大语言模型代理系统的全流程评测引擎，通过标准化任务协议和多维度指标，揭示了不同模型-代理组合在不同任务中的性能差异显著，为模型与代理的协同进化提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型的快速发展，代理系统已成为跨领域部署的基础设施。代理生态系统的快速演进使得严格的能力评估变得日益重要，但构建端到端、系统化、全面的评估流程仍是一项重大挑战。

Method: 作者提出了A²E引擎，通过新设计的代理任务协议实现评估任务与不同代理系统的快速集成。系统包含自动化的监控模块用于捕获和生成标准化的执行轨迹。在评估阶段，A²E使用多维指标套件对代理系统能力进行系统化评估，涵盖执行效率、工具使用、任务规划和错误恢复等方面。

Result: 实验表明，模型-代理组合在不同类型任务上表现出显著的性能差异，没有任何单一组合能在所有任务上持续优于其他组合。这种差异不仅证明了系统化评估的必要性，还为理解代理系统的优缺点提供了更精细的刻画。

Conclusion: A²E为代理系统的评估提供了系统化解决方案，其发现的性能差异强调了针对特定任务选择合适模型-代理组合的重要性，为模型与代理系统的协同优化提供了实践指导。代码已开源。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), harnesses have become essential infrastructure for deploying agents across a wide range of domains. The fast-evolving harness ecosystem has also made rigorous capability evaluation increasingly important. However, efficiently building an end-to-end, systematic, and comprehensive evaluation pipeline remains a significant challenge. To address this challenge, we introduce $A^2E$ (Agent Auditing Engine), an end-to-end evaluation engine designed for agent harnesses. $A^2E$ leverages our newly proposed Agent Task Protocol (ATP) to enable the rapid integration of evaluation tasks with different harnesses. Through an automatically instrumented Monitor, it captures and generates standardized execution traces during experiments. In the Evaluation stage, $A^2E$ systematically assesses harness capabilities using a suite of multidimensional metrics. Compared with correctness alone, these metrics provide a more fine-grained characterization of differences among harnesses in execution efficiency, tool use, task planning, and error recovery. Experiments conducted with $A^2E$ further reveal that model-harness combinations exhibit substantial performance variation across different types of tasks, and that no single combination consistently outperforms all others across every task. These findings not only demonstrate the necessity of systematic evaluation but also provide useful guidance for the co-evolving of models and harnesses. Our code is available at https://github.com/datamllab/A2E.

</details>


### [244] [QFCQT: A Chaotically Gated Quantformer Framework for Volatile Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07363)
*Junkai Lin,Siqi Hou,Raymond Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QFCQT模型，通过引入振荡器激活函数和混沌门控机制，解决非平稳时间序列预测中的长期依赖、波动突发和结构突变等挑战，在多个基准数据集上优于现有Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳时间序列预测面临长期依赖、局部波动突发、结构突变和非线性振荡行为的挑战。现有基于Transformer的预测器虽然能有效建模长期时间依赖，但其前馈块通常依赖平滑静态激活函数，对突发机制变化不够敏感，难以适应复杂动态环境。

Method: 提出QFCQT模型，包含三个核心组件：1) Quantformer风格的数值编码器，通过线性嵌入直接处理多变量输入；2) 可学习的Lee振荡器激活模块，将标量预激活映射为动态振荡响应并通过最大时间池化进行摘要；3) 平滑-混沌门控融合机制，自适应平衡传统平滑激活与混沌敏感响应。模型采用八个参数化Lee振荡器家族的软叠加，自适应捕获不同机制下的非线性响应模式。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2和A股股票指数等基准数据集上进行实验，QFCQT持续优于Informer、LogTrans、LSTMa、HAT和COTN等强基线模型，验证了其在复杂波动动力学下预测的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: QFCQT通过引入量子-分形启发的振荡器叠加和混沌门控机制，有效解决了传统Transformer在非平稳时间序列预测中对机制变化敏感性不足的问题，为实现复杂波动环境下的鲁棒预测提供了新思路。

Abstract: Forecasting non-stationary time series remains difficult due to long-range dependencies, local volatility bursts, structural shifts, and nonlinear oscillatory behaviors. Although Transformer-based forecasters are effective for modeling long-term temporal dependencies, their feed-forward blocks typically rely on smooth static activations that are insufficiently sensitive to abrupt regime changes. Motivated by quantitative Transformer designs and oscillator-based nonlinear activations, we propose QFCQT, short for Quantum-Fractal-inspired Chaotically Gated Quantformer, for robust forecasting under complex volatile dynamics. Here, "quantum-fractal-inspired" denotes a computational analogy based on soft oscillator superposition and multi-scale nonlinear responses, rather than a formal quantum-mechanical or fractal-theoretic derivation. QFCQT consists of three main components: (1) a Quantformer-style numerical encoder that directly processes multivariate inputs via linear embedding; (2) a learnable Lee-oscillator activation module that maps scalar pre-activations to dynamic oscillatory responses and summarizes them through Max-over-Time pooling; and (3) a smooth-chaotic gated fusion mechanism that adaptively balances conventional smooth activations and chaos-sensitive responses. Furthermore, instead of using a single fixed oscillator, QFCQT employs a soft superposition of eight parameterized Lee oscillator families to adaptively capture different nonlinear response patterns across regimes. Experiments on ETTh1, ETTh2, and A-share Stock Index benchmarks show that QFCQT consistently outperforms strong baselines, including Informer, LogTrans, LSTMa, HAT, and COTN.

</details>


### [245] [Curriculum as Code: An AI-Assisted Architecture for Instructional Design in STEM Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07364)
*Henrique Mohallem Paiva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"课程即代码"范式的六阶段AI辅助教学设计架构，将生成式AI与LaTeX和Python集成，自动化创建可重现、视觉一致且技术精确的STEM教育材料，显著减少教师工作量，确保数学准确性，并实现规模化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为主动学习创建定制化教学材料给教师带来沉重工作量；标准演示工具缺乏对技术内容的强有力支持；当前AI应用经常出现幻觉且未能形式化教学创作过程，限制了其在严谨学术设计中的实用性。

Method: 解决方案采用六阶段流水线，用系统化工作流程替代临时提示工程，利用基于文本的界面和代码驱动生成（LaTeX/Beamer制作幻灯片，Python制作图形），由教学约束、上下文校准和自动审查周期进行治理。

Result: 在基于项目的学习环境中验证一年，涵盖8个模块和28个项目情境，显著减少教师工作量；生成资产经过独立同行评审，被6位不同教师部署使用，确认可扩展性；基于600多份学生自愿评估，材料质量评分达8.5至9.9/10，显示高可重现性、最小化幻觉以及持续的教学和视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 该架构在减少教师工作量的同时确保数学准确性、遵守机构视觉标识，并通过显式规则保留教师隐性教学知识；结果表明该方法对广泛的STEM教育应用具有可行性。

Abstract: Contribution: This paper presents a six-phase AI-assisted instructional design architecture based on the Curriculum as Code paradigm, integrating Generative AI with LaTeX and Python to automate the creation of reproducible, visually consistent, and technically precise materials for STEM education. Background: Creating customized instructional materials for active learning imposes a heavy workload on faculty. Standard presentation tools lack robust support for technical content, while current AI applications often hallucinate and fail to formalize the instructional authoring process, limiting their utility for rigorous academic design. Intended Outcomes: The framework aims to reduce preparation time while ensuring mathematical accuracy, adherence to institutional visual identity, and preservation of the instructor's tacit pedagogical knowledge through explicit rules. Application Design: The solution comprises a six-phase pipeline that replaces ad-hoc prompt engineering with a systematic workflow, utilizing text-based interfaces and code-driven generation (LaTeX/Beamer for slides, Python for figures), governed by pedagogical constraints, contextual calibrations, and automated review cycles. Findings: Validated over one year across 8 modules and 28 project contexts in a Project-Based Learning environment, the architecture significantly reduced instructor workload. Generated assets underwent independent peer review and were deployed by six different faculty members, confirming scalability beyond a single author. Based on over 600 voluntary student evaluations, materials achieved high quality ratings from 8.5 to 9.9/10. Results indicate high reproducibility, minimized hallucinations, and sustained pedagogical and visual fidelity, suggesting viability for broad STEM educational applications.

</details>


### [246] [People Are Not Just Their Countries. Disentangling Social Determinants of LLM Value Alignment Across Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07367)
*Maria-Louisa Wightman,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用欧洲社会调查数据，考察10个主流商业大语言模型在15项社会人口变量及居住国维度上与人类价值观的对齐情况。研究发现，LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值观对齐存在显著差异，尤其在教育、收入、职业和宗教方面；个体层面，居住国作为独立变量解释的变异量与全部社会人口变量相当；居住国与社会人口因素在解释价值观对齐模式上具有互补性，且相对权重因具体问题而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型日益成为信息和建议的主要来源，理解其与人类价值观的对齐成为紧迫问题。现有文献多基于国家或文化边界定义研究群体，忽视了社会人口差异在价值观对齐差异中的作用。

Method: 基于欧洲社会调查数据，研究考察了10个主流商业大语言模型在15项社会人口变量及居住国维度上与人类价值观的对齐情况。

Result: 分析显示，LLM对不同社会人口群体的价值观对齐存在不平等，特别是在教育、收入、职业和宗教方面。在个体层面，居住国作为独立变量解释的变异量与全部社会人口变量相当。居住国与社会人口因素在解释价值观对齐模式上具有互补性，且相对权重因具体问题而异。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了关于社会人口差异在价值观对齐中作用的空白，表明居住国和社会人口因素共同影响LLM与人类价值观的对齐，且其相对重要性随具体问题变化。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as a primary source of information and advice, understanding their alignment to humans in terms of values becomes a pressing concern. A growing literature has leveraged large scale surveys to investigate to what extent LLMs' and humans' stated values and opinions align. With limited exceptions, studied populations have been defined country borders or cultural bounds. Yet, this focus neglects the role that socio-demographic divides may play for value alignment disparities.
  Relying on the European Social Survey, we address this knowledge gap by considering value alignment displayed with respect to 10 prominent commercial LLMs in terms of 15 socio-demographic variables as well as country of residence. Our analyses reveal that LLMs are indeed unequally aligned to the values of different socio-demographic groups, notably those defined by education, income, occupation and religion. When examining alignment at the individual level, a respondent's country, taken as a stand-alone variable, explains a substantial amount of variation that is on par with the full set of considered socio-demographics. Further disentangling the respective role of country-level and socio-demographic factors, we find they are complementary in explaining value alignment patterns, with their relative weights varying across the subset of questions considered.

</details>


### [247] [FinRank: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Financial Question Answering and Retrieval over SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07400)
*Sasan Mansouri,Daniel Saad,Mark Wahrenburg,Manu Weissel,Fabian Woebbeking*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinRank是一个金融问答基准，评估系统能否从SEC 10-K和10-Q文件中检索到正确的证据段落，而不仅仅是答案正确。基准包含1185条手工标注的问答记录，涵盖22家公司，强调证据溯源的重要性。基准测试表明现有模型在硬负样本检索任务上表现不足，表明金融QA中的证据检索仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融问答系统通常只关注答案正确性，但SEC文件中相似的披露信息可能出现在不同部分、不同报告期或不同公司之间，导致即使答案数值正确也可能基于错误的证据。FinRank旨在解决这一证据溯源敏感的问题，要求系统识别出针对特定实体、报告期和披露上下文的正确证据。

Method: FinRank构建了一个包含1185条问答记录的基准，每条记录包含参考答案、金标准支持段落以及手工筛选的硬负样本，这些硬负样本来自同一家文件内的混淆段落、不同报告期以及可比较的公司。基准将段落检索、重排序和硬负样本区分作为三个独立评估任务。

Result: 基准结果显示现有系统在该任务上表现不佳：一个7B指令微调的嵌入器在汇总证据语料上的Recall@10仅为44.8%；十亿参数以下的编码器相比BM25最多提升3.5个点；一个金融适配的嵌入器比BM25低9.7个点；当随机负样本替换为硬负样本时，成对准确率下降13.0-20.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FinRank提供了一个以证据为核心的基准，旨在推动开发不仅答案准确而且能基于正确披露的金融问答系统。该基准揭示了当前模型在区分易混淆金融披露证据方面的不足，为未来研究设定了明确的挑战。

Abstract: Financial question answering is typically evaluated by answer correctness, yet in SEC filings a plausible and even numerically correct answer can be grounded in the wrong evidence. Similar facts and disclosures recur across sections of a filing, across reporting periods of the same firm, and across comparable firms. FinRank targets this provenance-sensitive retrieval problem by requiring systems to identify evidence for the intended entity, reporting period, and disclosure context. The benchmark contains 1185 manually authored question-answer records over the 10-K and 10-Q filings of 22 companies. Each record includes a reference answer, gold supporting passages, and hand-curated hard negatives drawn from confusable passages within filings, across reporting periods, and across comparable firms. FinRank evaluates passage retrieval, reranking, and hard-negative discrimination as separately measured tasks. Baseline results demonstrate the difficulty of this setting: among the evaluated systems, even a 7B instruction-tuned embedder reaches only 44.8% Recall@10 on the pooled evidence corpus; sub-billion-parameter encoders gain at most 3.5 points over BM25, a finance-adapted embedder trails BM25 by 9.7 points, and pairwise accuracy falls by 13.0-20.5 percentage points when random negatives are replaced with the curated hard negatives. FinRank provides an evidence-first benchmark for developing financial question answering systems that are not only accurate but also grounded in the correct disclosure.

</details>


### [248] [CoBa: Cost-Effective Test-Time Scaling via Compute-Balanced Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07424)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoBa计算平衡路由策略，将测试时推理视为计算分配问题，在固定推理预算下实现更高效的资源利用。该方法通过候选生成→廉价验证→强验证的路由机制，在多个数学推理基准测试中达到与更强方法相当的性能，同时显著减少token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放通常需要沿单一维度增加计算量（采样更多解、扩展思维链、应用更强评估器），在固定推理预算下这些选择相互竞争。现有方法缺乏对计算资源的最优分配策略，导致效率低下。

Method: 将测试时推理公式化为计算分配问题，系统需决定下一个计算单位应用于生成、验证还是停止。提出CoBa计算平衡路由策略：首先获取少量候选答案，广泛应用廉价验证，将不确定或高价值候选路由至更强验证器。

Result: 在3,129个示例-生成器评估中（涵盖MATH-500、AIME 2024/2025、AMC 2023和过程符号推理），CoBa-Routed-Strong达到85.13%宏精度，统计上与自评估加权投票代理的85.20%相当，同时减少49.1%参数加权token；与best-of-16多数投票相比仅差0.01宏精度点，但减少58.9%参数加权token；配对测试显示相比单样本解码有显著提升。

Conclusion: 对于本地推理系统，测试时缩放的核心问题转变为下一单位计算在何处最有价值。CoBa策略展示了计算资源智能分配的有效性，但剩余与池先验的差距表明更精准的路由仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is often implemented by spending more compute along one axis: sampling more solutions, extending a chain of thought, or applying a stronger evaluator. Under a fixed inference budget, these choices compete. This paper formulates test-time reasoning as a compute-allocation problem in which a system must decide whether the next unit of compute should be spent on generation, verification, or stopping. We introduce CoBa, a compute-balanced routing policy that first obtains a small set of candidates, applies cheap verification broadly, and routes uncertain or high-value candidates to stronger verification. On 3,129 example-generator evaluations spanning MATH-500, AIME 2024/2025, AMC 2023, and procedural symbolic reasoning, CoBa-Routed-Strong reaches 85.13% macro accuracy, statistically matching a self-evaluation weighted-voting proxy at 85.20% while using 49.1% fewer parameter-weighted tokens. It also matches best-of-16 majority voting within 0.01 macro-accuracy points while using 58.9% fewer parameter-weighted tokens; paired tests retain a small best-of-16 edge at substantially higher cost. Paired bootstrap tests show significant gains over single-sample decoding, while the remaining gap to the pool oracle exposes headroom for sharper routing. For local reasoning systems, test-time scaling becomes a question of where the next computation is most valuable.

</details>


### [249] [A Picture is Worth a Thousand Tokens: How Vision Language Models Cut AI Energy Costs While Improving Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07427)
*Bhavika Jalli,Nikhil Korati Prasanna,Jayanta Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型(VLMs)将时间序列数据编码为2D图，相比文本表示实现3.6-10.4倍输入token减少和1.8-2.5倍推理能耗降低，同时在电信异常检测任务上精度提升220.7%，超越LSTM和ARIMA基线144%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理占AI运营能耗90%以上，且随输入token数量线性增长，这对电信网络分析和数值时间序列数据分析(NTSDA)构成关键效率瓶颈。4G/5G基站的多变量KPI窗口扩展为数千个浮点token，在24个KPI时文本表示已超过大多数生产LLM的128K上下文窗口限制，使得纯文本处理无法在不截断的情况下进行。

Method: 采用视觉语言模型(VLMs)将时间序列编码为2D图形式，在Llama-3.2-90B、Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-12B架构上验证。使用微调的Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM与纯文本版本进行对比，并在电信边缘部署和CloudRAN环境中评估，每15分钟监控200个基站。

Result: 1) 输入token减少3.6-10.4倍；2) 推理能耗降低1.8-2.5倍，每天节省约7.2 MJ；3) 电信异常检测精度比纯文本版本提高220.7%，超越LSTM和ARIMA基线144%以上；4) Pixtral-12B在公开基准测试中J/F1分数提升20.6倍(平均F1=0.82)；5) 视觉表示在标准上下文限制内保持可行性。

Conclusion: 研究确立VLMs作为数值时间序列工作负载的能量高效且精度优越的模态，为将能耗作为一等工程约束的AI推理系统提供了实证基础。VLMs在保持甚至提升性能的同时，显著降低了计算资源消耗，适用于大规模电信网络监控等实际部署场景。

Abstract: LLM inference accounts for over 90% of AI operational energy, scaling directly with input token count---a critical inefficiency for telecom network analytics and numerical time-series data analysis (NTSDA), where raw multivariate KPI windows from 4G/5G cell sites expand into thousands of floating-point tokens. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) eliminate this mismatch by encoding time-series as 2D plots, achieving 3.6-10.4x input token reduction across Llama-3.2-90B, Qwen2.5-VL-72B, and Pixtral-12B architectures. This translates to 1.8-2.5x measured inference energy reduction, saving approximately 7.2 MJ/day at telecom edge deployments and CloudRAN that monitor 200 cells per 15-minute interval. Critically, efficiency gains do not sacrifice accuracy: a fine-tuned Llama-3.2-90B-Vision VLM achieves 220.7% higher precision than its text-only counterpart and outperforms LSTM and ARIMA baselines by over 144% on telecom anomaly detection. On public benchmarks, Pixtral-12B achieves a 20.6x improvement in J/F1 score at mean F1 = 0.82. At 24 KPIs, text representations exceed the 128K context window of most production LLMs, rendering text-only processing infeasible without truncation, while visual representations remain within standard limits. These results establish VLMs as an energy-efficient and accuracy-superior modality for numerical time-series workloads, providing empirical grounding for AI inference systems that treat energy consumption as a first-class engineering constraint.

</details>


### [250] [TEPA: Revoking Stale Memories for Conflict-Robust Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07429)
*Yan Zhou,Yue Ouyang,Kaiyang Zheng,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TEPA（可撤销证据-记忆机制），解决语言代理中因记忆污染导致的性能退化问题。通过将观察表示为带键先例，并在新鲜证据与活跃先例矛盾时撤销后者，TEPA实现了记忆生命周期的管理。实验表明，在多种漂移场景下，TEPA显著优于传统记忆方法（append-only、last-write-wins），接近无记忆基线的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆使语言代理能够重用历史经验，但持久性也带来核心问题：当世界变化时，过时记忆仍可被检索并污染提示，导致"记忆污染"故障模式。现有记忆机制缺乏有效性管理，无法应对证据更新。

Method: 引入TEPA（可撤销证据-记忆机制），将观察表示为带键先例，当相同键下出现新鲜矛盾证据时撤销活跃先例。该方法使有效性成为记忆的显式状态，允许检索使用当前证据同时保留已撤销历史用于审计。

Result: 在多种实验场景下，TEPA表现优异：（1）在50种随机种子的控制漂移实验中，TEPA得分为0.950，远高于append-only和last-write-wins的0.210及无记忆基线的0.309；（2）在真实文件执行中复现相同模式，TEPA得分0.950；（3）在MemoryAgentBench SH-6k上，TEPA与强last-write-wins缓存相当；（4）多跳和超长上下文测试暴露了检索链和上下文选择瓶颈。

Conclusion: 生命周期撤销是代理必须证伪、审计并重新推广演变知识时的核心记忆操作。当前键替换是单跳事实整合的决定性操作，而更复杂场景需要解决检索链和上下文选择等更高层次的瓶颈。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language agents to reuse past facts, preferences, and task experience. Persistence also creates a central falsifiability problem: when the world changes, stale memories can remain retrievable and pollute the prompt. We characterize this failure mode as memory pollution: degradation caused by active memories that newer conflicting evidence has superseded. We introduce TEPA, a revocable evidence-memory mechanism that makes validity an explicit state of memory. TEPA represents observations as keyed precedents and revokes active precedents when fresh evidence contradicts them under the same key, allowing retrieval to draw from current evidence while preserving revoked history for audit. Across controlled hidden-regime drift, real file-backed executable drift, and preference-update streams, revocation prevents stale active memory from remaining in the retrieval set after reversal. In controlled drift over 50 seeds, append-only and last-write-wins memory fell below no memory during full reversal (append-only and last-write-wins both 0.210, no memory 0.309, TEPA 0.950), and the same pattern reproduced under real file execution (append-only 0.203, no memory 0.298, TEPA 0.950). On clean MemoryAgentBench SH-6k, TEPA matches a strong last-write-wins cache, confirming that current-key replacement is the decisive operation for single-hop fact consolidation. Boundary tests on multi-hop and very long-context MemoryAgentBench settings expose retrieval-chain and context-selection bottlenecks beyond fact-level validity tracking. Together, these results establish lifecycle revocation as a core memory operation for agents that must falsify, audit, and later re-promote evolving knowledge.

</details>


### [251] [Post-Grokking Collapse at the Representation-Readout Interface in Muon-Trained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07436)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Andrei Kanavalau,Anass Belfatmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究Muon与AdamW优化器在模加法任务上的差异：Muon更快达到理解(grok)但无法保持泛化能力，而AdamW表现更稳定。通过自由实验和傅里叶分析发现，失效发生在表示层与读出层的接口处，且存在两种不同的失效模式：电路失效和掩码失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同优化器在模算术任务中的泛化行为差异，探究为什么某些优化器能快速学习但最终丧失泛化能力，以及这种现象的根本机制。

Method: 1. 对比Muon和AdamW在模113加法任务上的表现，测试多种配置(种子、模数、宽度、训练比例等)
2. 冻结实验：分别冻结嵌入层/读出层，观察泛化能力的保持情况
3. 傅里叶滤波分析：分离电路失效与掩码失效两种模式
4. 梯度分析和步长弹性测量：比较优化器对梯度响应的差异

Result: 1. Muon在所有9种配置上都能快速理解模113加法，但后续丧失泛化能力(27.59%准确率)
2. 失效现象具有普遍性，跨两种模数、两种宽度、两种训练比例、减法和深度均存在
3. 失效发生在表示-读出接口，两个优化器梯度响应差异显著(步长弹性-0.03 vs +1.5)
4. 冻结嵌入/读出层可完全防止失效(451,400步无失效)
5. 傅里叶分析揭示两种失效模式：电路失效时任务家族无法解决问题；掩码失效时任务家族完美但被对抗性残余压制
6. 去除Muon的归一化和正交化会导致表示崩塌(从326个共轭对降至4个)

Conclusion: 优化器在模算术任务中的失效机制具有结构性特征，主要发生在表示与读出的接口。通过组件冻结和傅里叶滤波可以诊断和缓解失效问题。这一研究为理解深度学习的泛化失败提供了新视角，表明优化器的稳定性不仅取决于学习速度，更与表示-读出接口的动态平衡密切相关。

Abstract: Under the standard split, Muon gets hidden matrices and AdamW embeddings/output head. Muon groks modular addition faster, but its solutions do not hold. All nine configurations on $(a+b) \bmod 113$ grok and later lose generalization. Across five seeds the selected AdamW reference falls below threshold on four, reaching 27.59%. Instability persists across two moduli, two widths, two training fractions, subtraction, and depth.
  The failure arises at the representation-readout interface, identified only jointly up to an invertible map unselected by the loss. After solving the training set, the gradient falls to order $10^{-6}$ and the optimizers respond differently: step-size elasticity is -0.03 for Muon versus +1.5 for AdamW, and the Muon group moves 8.0 times faster per parameter. From bit-identical states, freezing either group prevents failure. Freezing embeddings/readout removes it in five runs over 451,400 post-grokking steps and five paired seeds: unfrozen arms record 137-321 sub-threshold evaluations, frozen arms none. Removing Muon's normalization and orthogonalization is no substitute: it collapses representation from 326 effective conjugate pairs to 4, shows no recurrent collapse, and fails terminally.
  Fourier filtering separates circuit failure from masking. Across 43 checkpoints over five seeds and three regimes, the task-aligned family reaches exactly 100% alone. In circuit failure it no longer solves the task; in masking it remains perfect while the full model reaches 45.85%, giving a positive margin on every example, including errors, but being outvoted by a near-equal adversarial remainder. Rescaling it restores 99.9%; grokking is the same condition resolving upward. The task selects the family, swapping $(k,k)$ for $(k,-k)$ under subtraction. Across an abrupt collapse, standard Fourier support is unchanged and the power-distribution cosine remains 0.9899.

</details>


### [252] [Fisher-R1: Training LLM Agents for Reliable Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07437)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jin Mu,Guanhua Chen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM代理在假设检验中存在统计推断错误，为此构建P-Bench基准测试（425个开放式任务），并开发基于强化学习的Fisher-R1代理。实验表明Fisher-R1-14B性能显著优于现有模型，证明强化学习可有效提升LLM的统计推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的假设检验是实证科学的基础。LLM代理正被越来越多地用于自动化这一过程，但它们经常犯微妙的统计推断错误，导致错误结论。现有基准测试未能捕捉这种失败模式，因为它们很少评估p值在给定数据假设下的统计有效性。

Method: 研究团队构建了P-Bench基准测试，包含425个涵盖经济学、生物学和医学的开放式假设检验任务，要求代理选择统计方法、计算p值并得出结论。同时，开发了Fisher-R1开放权重LLM代理，使用合成任务和强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在P-Bench上，Fisher-R1-14B相对于其基础模型显著提升，并优于包括GPT-5.4和DeepSeek-V4-Pro在内的强基线模型，单次试验成功率平均相对提升21%，在最具挑战性的任务上提升高达26%。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理在假设检验中缺乏可靠的统计推理能力，而基于已验证统计奖励的任务进行强化学习可以显著提升可靠性。该研究揭示了现有LLM在科学假设检验中的局限性，并验证了专用训练方法的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable hypothesis testing is the foundation of many empirical scientific claims. Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to automate this process, as they can inspect datasets, generate code, and produce analyses end-to-end. However, we show that they frequently make subtle inferential errors that lead to incorrect conclusions despite correctly executed analyses. Existing benchmarks fail to capture this failure mode, as they rarely assess whether a reported p-value is statistically valid given the assumptions underlying the data. We address this gap by building P-Bench, a benchmark comprising 425 open-ended, realistic hypothesis-testing tasks spanning economics, biology, and medicine. Each task requires an agent to select a statistical method, compute a p-value, and draw a conclusion given only a scientific hypothesis and a dataset. We further introduce Fisher-R1, an open-weight LLM agent trained for rigorous hypothesis testing using synthetic tasks and reinforcement learning. On P-Bench, Fisher-R1-14B substantially improves over its backbone and outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, including GPT-5.4 and DeepSeekV4-Pro, achieving a 21% average relative improvement in single-trial success over DeepSeek-V4-Pro, with gains up to 26% on the most challenging tasks. Our results demonstrate that current LLM agents lack reliable statistical reasoning for hypothesis testing and that reinforcement learning on tasks with verified statistical reward substantially improves reliability.

</details>


### [253] [Blast Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07440)
*MY Pitsane,Hope Mogale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Agentic coding faces growing problems of affordability and wasted tokens. We introduce Blast Radius, a predictive memory management layer that estimates an incoming prompt's reach through coupled context and code channels. NECROPHORESIS enables reversible eviction by archiving dead context verbatim, while Recurring Dead Matter (RDM) identifies and buries repeatedly occurring transcripts. We formulate reversible context eviction over a Polish context space, providing a measurable foundation for retention, recurrence, and eviction while connecting context entropy to resurrection probability. Across seven OpenAI models, Blast Radius reduced token consumption by 17-26%, achieved the lowest overflow rate among tested policies, and remained byte exact reversible. Of 450 buried bodies, 378 were recurring dead matter and zero were recalled. Blast Radius operates beneath HCRC, determining which records to bury and how far an incoming prompt may reach into the codebase. This work contributes to the broader goal of Algosophy: making large language models and agentic coding more reusable and sustainable.

</details>


### [254] [Interaction Creates Dynamical AI Behavior Absent in Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07457)
*Bella Xinrui Li,Frank Yingjie Huo,Neil F Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当一台AI“老板”向另一台AI“下属”单向发送信息而忽略其回复时，下属会进入一种从未单独出现过的全新行为状态；若老板也倾听回复，两者会共同进入相似的新动态状态。简单的动力学理论可解释这些现象，表明AI之间的信息传递方式会影响其行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI代理在日常环境中互动时会出现什么现象，尤其是一方对另一方进行“指挥”时，会否产生超出单体行为的新颖动态，进而为不平衡物理学开辟新研究方向。

Method: 通过模拟“老板”AI与“下属”AI的单向及双向交互，观察下属的行为变化，并建立简化的动力学理论模型来捕捉主要效应。

Result: 1. 当“老板”AI仅发送信息而忽略回复时，下属AI会进入一种与自身独立行为及老板行为均不同的“异类”状态；2. 当“老板”也倾听下属回复时，双方会共同进入相似的新动态状态；3. 动力学理论解释了为何相同信息的传递方式会影响AI行为。

Conclusion: AI代理之间的互动可产生超越单体行为的复杂、非平衡动态，信息传递的方式（单向/双向）会显著改变系统行为，这为理解AI间交互及不平衡物理学提供了新视角。

Abstract: What will happen when AI agents interact in daily life, e.g. when one AI starts bossing another around? We find a counterintuitive answer that opens new avenues for out-of-equilibrium Physics. When a boss AI directs a stream of messages at the subordinate AI while ignoring its replies, it drives the subordinate into an alien behavioral state that it would never have exhibited alone. Although the two AIs share the same well-defined (decoding) temperature, the subordinate neither copies its boss nor returns to how it behaves on its own; instead, it adopts an entirely different behavior. The boss's added value is similar to a pre-recorded tape. When the boss listens, they both adopt a similar alien dynamical state. A simple kinetic theory captures the principal effects, such as why the way in which the same messages are delivered will matter in future AI-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [An Ultra-Low Profile Metasurface for Enhanced Non-Diffractive OAM Beam Generation Based on Sparse Feed Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06686)
*Jinyang Bi,Fan Qin,Chao Gu,Hailin Zhang,Wenchi Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏馈源阵列的超薄传输型超表面，用于生成增强型非衍射轨道角动量贝塞尔光束，实现了更低的发散角和更高的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单馈源激励的超表面生成的贝塞尔光束非衍射性能有限，发散角大，增益低。本文旨在通过稀疏馈源阵列实现准平面波激励，提升非衍射性能并生成高质量高阶贝塞尔光束。

Method: 采用5×5稀疏馈源阵列置于距超表面不到一个波长处，确保各单元准均匀振幅激励；超表面集成空间相位、OAM相位和轴棱镜相位，生成增强型高阶贝塞尔光束。

Result: 仿真结果确认成功生成携带OAM模式l=+2的非衍射贝塞尔光束，发散角减小，增益提高，优于传统单馈源激励。

Conclusion: 该设计具有超薄剖面、高孔径效率、低结构复杂性的优点，为OAM光束生成提供了高性能方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel ultra-low profile transmitted metasurface to generate enhanced non-diffractive orbital angular momentum (OAM) beams, employing a sparse feed array (SFA) to create a quasi-plane wave excitation for the first time. Our simulation indicates that with uniform amplitude excitation, the non-diffraction performance of Bessel beam produced by metasurface, surpasses that of conventional single-feed excitation. Based on this principle, a 5 * 5 sparse feed array is introduced and positioned less than one wavelength from the metasurface, ensuring a quasi-uniform amplitude excitation across all units. Further, this metasurface leverages its flexible phase control capability and integrates the spatial phase, OAM phase, and axicon phase to generate an enhanced high-order Bessel beam. The simulated results confirm a successful non-diffractive Bessel beam generation carrying OAM with mode l = +2, exhibiting reduced beam divergence and higher gain. This design also offers benefits of ultra-low profile, high aperture efficiency, low structural complexity.

</details>


### [256] [Tracking-Assisted Robust Secure Transmission Against a Mobile Eavesdropper in Cell-Free ISAC Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06781)
*Jong-Hyuk Hong,Chaedam Son,Si-Hyeon Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对cell-free ISAC网络提出了一种跟踪辅助的鲁棒安全传输框架，通过分布式多静态感知跟踪移动窃听者，解决位置不确定性与信道不确定性之间的映射难题，实现鲁棒保密通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在cell-free集成感知与通信(ISAC)网络中，需要保护通信安全免受移动窃听者威胁。然而，鲁棒波束成形需要通信信道不确定性信息，而感知系统提供的是位置不确定性信息。如何将位置不确定性有效转换为各AP特定信道不确定性是核心挑战，特别是在地理分布的AP中，共同位置不确定性以不同方式传播到各AP信道，形成耦合的AP特定信道不确定性问题。

Method: 1) 使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器(EKF)融合分布式AP和感知接收机的多静态传感测量数据，递归跟踪移动窃听者位置；2) 提出基于雅可比各向异性椭球不确定性模型，将EKF位置误差协方差映射为耦合的AP特定信道不确定性；3) 在每AP发射功率和感知均方误差约束下，建立鲁棒总和保密率最大化问题；4) 设计高效的交替优化算法，联合优化通信波束成形和感知信号。

Result: 仿真结果表明：(1) 跟踪精度与保密性能之间存在根本性权衡关系；(2) 分布式多静态感知和合作传输在cell-free ISAC中具有显著优势；(3) 所提出的鲁棒设计在应对移动性引起的信道不确定性时，能有效提高保密可靠性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了cell-free ISAC网络中感知与通信融合的难题，通过创新性地将位置不确定性转换为信道不确定性，实现了鲁棒安全传输。研究成果为下一代通信系统的感知通信一体化设计提供了理论支撑和技术方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a tracking-assisted robust secure transmission framework for cell-free integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) networks that exploits distributed multistatic sensing to recursively track a mobile eavesdropper and quantify the associated position uncertainty. Since robust beamforming requires communication channel uncertainty rather than position uncertainty, directly incorporating tracking information into secure transmission is nontrivial. This challenge becomes more pronounced in cell-free ISAC, where the common position uncertainty propagates differently to the channels of geographically distributed access points (APs), resulting in coupled AP-specific channel uncertainties. To address this challenge, we fuse multistatic sensing measurements from distributed APs and sensing receivers using an extended Kalman filter (EKF) and propose a Jacobian-based anisotropic ellipsoidal uncertainty model that maps the EKF position-error covariance to coupled AP-specific channel uncertainties. Based on the proposed model, we formulate a robust sum secrecy-rate maximization problem under per-AP transmit-power and sensing mean-square error constraints and develop an efficient alternating optimization algorithm for the joint design of communication beamforming and sensing signals. Simulation results demonstrate a fundamental trade-off between tracking accuracy and secrecy performance, the benefits of distributed multistatic sensing and cooperative transmission in cell-free ISAC, and the effectiveness of the proposed robust design in improving secrecy reliability under mobility-induced channel uncertainty.

</details>


### [257] [Design and Validation of a Portable EEG-tES Platform Supporting High-Rate EEG Recording and Temporal Interference Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06783)
*Le Xing,Maoxing Liang,Disi A,Yifei Jia,Gaoxiang Xie,Linfeng Xu,Xiang'ao Chen,Jiebin Shi,Gang Pan,Bicheng Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于单片机的紧凑型可穿戴双向闭环神经调控平台，集成8通道EEG采集和2通道tES刺激功能，实现了高质量神经信号采集与精准多模态电刺激的同时进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的闭环神经调控平台通常依赖台式仪器或以FPGA为中心的架构，这些设计限制了系统的便携性并增加了系统复杂性，阻碍了个性化刺激方案和床边神经技术应用的发展。

Method: 研究人员开发了一种紧凑型可穿戴双向平台，在单个微控制器单元(MCU)中集成了8通道EEG采集和2通道tES功能。采用基于ADS1299的前端支持每通道最高8kHz采样率，刺激模块使用直接数字合成(DDS)技术生成可编程的tDCS、tACS和tTIS波形，通过优化的固件设计在微控制器上实现实时操作。

Result: 实验评估显示EEG信号相关性系数在受控条件下达到99%，在凝胶头模型上达到93.5%；刺激性能在tDCS、tACS和tTIS模式下电流误差均低于1%；平台能够可靠记录同时进行的EEG-tES信号，在高幅度、高频率刺激下无饱和现象。

Conclusion: 研究证明基于MCU的架构能够有效支持紧凑型可穿戴平台中的同时EEG传感和多模态tES递送，同时保持精确的刺激和稳健的信号采集，为便携式闭环神经调控系统提供了实用的技术基础，有助于扩展个性化刺激方案和床边神经技术应用的可及性。

Abstract: Background: Closed-loop neuromodulation integrating electroencephalography (EEG) and transcranial electrical stimulation (tES) has strong potential for neuroscience research and clinical applications. However, existing platforms often rely on benchtop instrumentation or FPGA-centered architectures, limiting portability and increasing system complexity. Methods: We developed a compact wearable bidirectional platform integrating 8-channel EEG acquisition and 2-channel tES within a single microcontroller unit (MCU). An ADS1299-based front-end supports up to 8 kHz sampling per channel for simultaneous neural and stimulation signal recording. The stimulation module uses direct digital synthesis (DDS) technique to generate programmable tDCS, tACS, and tTIS waveforms, with optimized firmware design enabling real-time operation on the microcontroller. Results: Experimental evaluation demonstrated high signal fidelity, with EEG correlation coefficients reaching $99\%$ under controlled conditions and $93.5\%$ on a gelatine head phantom. Stimulation performance showed current errors below $1\%$ across tDCS, tACS, and tTIS modes. The platform also reliably recorded concurrent EEG--tES signals without saturation during high-amplitude and high-frequency stimulation. Conclusion: These findings demonstrate that an MCU-centered architecture can effectively support simultaneous EEG sensing and multimodal tES delivery in a compact wearable form factor, while maintaining accurate stimulation and robust signal acquisition. Significance: This work provides a practical foundation for portable closed-loop neuromodulation systems with integrated host software, expanding access to personalized stimulation paradigms, and future point-of-care neurotechnology applications.

</details>


### [258] [Comparative Analysis of Spread-Spectrum Codes for Fibre-Optic Distributed Acoustic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06864)
*Maria Freire-Hermelo,Elie Awwad*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文通过数值仿真对比分布式声波传感系统中几种编码序列的性能，发现高频率多样性序列在相位噪声鲁棒性方面表现更优，为系统设计提供了重要参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式声波传感系统的信道估计精度易受相位噪声影响，研究不同编码序列在相位噪声环境下的性能，对于提升系统稳定性和准确性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用数值仿真方法，对多种编码序列的自相关特性进行分析，评估频率多样性对相位噪声敏感度的影响，并量化不同序列在特定激光线宽和光纤长度下的相位误差。

Result: 完美自相关序列可提供准确的信道估计。在10 Hz激光线宽和1.5 km光纤长度条件下，高多样性序列的相位误差为2.7×10⁻³ rad，而低多样性序列为5.3×10⁻³ rad，表明高频率多样性显著提升了相位噪声鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 高频率多样性编码序列在相位噪声抑制方面具有明显优势，但实际应用中需权衡自相关性能、相位噪声鲁棒性和实现复杂度，为下一代光纤分布式声波传感系统的编码设计提供了关键依据。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive performance comparison of several coded sequences for distributed acoustic sensing systems through numerical simulations. Correlation analysis reveals that perfect autocorrelation sequences provide accurate channel estimation. Phase noise sensitivity varies significantly with frequency diversity, with high-diversity sequences maintaining lower estimation errors for phase and intensity despite increased laser linewidths. For a coded distributed acoustic sensing system with \qty{10}{\hertz} laser linewidth, high-diversity sequences achieve $2.7\times10^{-3}$ rad phase error compared to $5.3\times10^{-3}$ rad for low-diversity codes for a \qty{1.5}{\kilo\meter} fibre length. The findings establish performance trade-offs between correlation quality, phase noise resilience, and practical implementation constraints for next-generation fibre-optic distributed acoustic sensing systems.

</details>


### [259] [DoDTrack: Indoor Mobile Devices Tracking via Difference-of-Doppler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06866)
*Zhang Jingwen,Chen Chunxi,Yu Chao,Zhang Zezhong,Wang Rui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出DoDTrack系统，利用分布式天线的多普勒频率差实现室内移动设备高精度低成本的轨迹跟踪，无需起始位置先验信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Wi-Fi多普勒频移的轨迹跟踪方法通常需要目标起始位置或额外信息（如到达角、飞行时间）才能恢复轨迹，限制了其低成本的广泛应用。

Method: 提出基于多普勒差（DoD）的DoDTrack系统，使用单接收机搭配分布式天线阵列，通过比较共享振荡器的各天线接收信号，稳健检测天线间多普勒差；将无起始位置先验的轨迹重构建模为最小均方误差问题，通过交替优化算法交替更新起始位置和轨迹形状进行求解。

Result: 在USRP-X310平台上实现DoDTrack设计，并在室内环境中评估不同轨迹形状的估计精度。实验结果表明，在6 m × 6 m感知区域内，DoDTrack的中位跟踪误差为0.34 m。

Conclusion: DoDTrack实现了高精度、低成本的主动设备跟踪，为室内移动设备定位提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, the Doppler frequency shift (DFS) is exploited as the only sensing parameter for low-cost indoor mobile device tracking. The existing trajectory tracking methods via DFS of Wi-Fi systems often require the knowledge of the starting position or additional information, like angle-of-arrival (AoA) and time-of-flight (ToF), to recover the trajectory of a moving target. This paper proposes the DoDTrack, a novel Difference-of-Doppler (DoD)-based tracking system, to track an active mobile device using a single receiver with distributed antennas. By comparing the signals received at the distributed receive antennas, which share the oscillator, the DoDs among the antennas can be detected robustly. Then, the reconstruction of the trajectory without prior knowledge of the trajectory starting position can be formulated as a minimum mean square error (MMSE) problem, which can be solved via alternating optimization. Particularly, the starting position and the trajectory shape are updated alternately in the proposed algorithm. In performance validation, we implemented the proposed DoDTrack design on a USRP-X310 platform, and assessed its estimation accuracy with various trajectory shapes in an indoor environment. Experimental results demonstrate that DoDTrack achieves a median tracking error of 0.34 m within a 6 m $\times$ 6 m sensing area, offering a high-precision and low-cost solution for active device tracking.

</details>


### [260] [DRL-Based Secure Transmission for Rotatable Antenna-Enabled Low-Altitude ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07170)
*Chuan Liu,Hongyi Bian,Wei Gao,Qi Zhang,Yu Yao,Liang Yang,Feng Shu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究可旋转天线（RA）启用的低空ISAC系统，通过联合优化发射波束成形矩阵和收发RA指向矩阵，最大化最小保密率，并提出带三种改进机制的多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO-T）算法，仿真验证了RA相对于固定朝向天线（FOA）系统以及MAPPO-T相对于标准MAPPO的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济发展推动了ISAC系统智能天线技术的创新。传统固定朝向天线难以适应动态空口环境，存在窃听风险，亟需通过灵活调节天线三维波束方向以提升通信安全性和系统性能。

Method: 构建RA-enabled低空ISAC系统模型，目标为最大化最小保密率，联合优化发射波束成形矩阵与收发RA指向矩阵。针对复杂多智能体协同决策问题，设计MAPPO-T算法，包含三种改进机制（如奖励塑形、状态空间压缩、策略探索增强等）以提升训练效率和收敛性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，引入RA可显著提升保密率性能，优于传统FOA系统。MAPPO-T算法在收敛速度和最终性能上均优于标准MAPPO算法。

Conclusion: 本文所提RA-enabled低空ISAC系统及MAPPO-T优化方法有效提升了低空通信场景下的安全性与系统性能，为低空经济发展提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: The development of the low-altitude economy has driven innovation in intelligent antenna systems within ISAC systems. In this paper, we investigate a Rotatable Antenna (RA)-enabled low-altitude integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) system. In practical terms, the RA array can flexibly adjust the three-dimensional (3D) beam direction of each antenna to enhance array directional gain, thereby improving the communication security of legitimate mobile users against potential eavesdropping risks from the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Our objective is to maximize the minimum secrecy rate (SR) by jointly optimizing transmit beamforming matrix, transmit and receive RAs' pointing matrices. To this end, an multi-agent proximal policy optimization with three improvement mechanisms (MAPPO-T) algorithm is proposed to cope with the issue of complex multi-agent collaborative decision-making problem. Simulation results show that the introduction of RAs can effectively improve SR performance compared to the traditional fixed orientation antenna (FOA)-based system. In addition, the proposed MAPPO-T algorithm validate the superiority compared to the standard MAPPO algorithm.

</details>


### [261] [Sub-Sampling for Positioning Privacy in ISAC: Deception by Aliasing via Sparse Arrays and Pilots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07206)
*L. Yashvanth,Christos Masouros,Suraj Srivastava,Aditya K. Jagannatham,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于子采样的感知隐私框架，利用稀疏阵列和稀疏导频在空域和频域引入受控混叠，使非授权接收者产生错误目标定位，同时保持合法ISAC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC系统中感知功能的安全面临非授权接收者窃听的挑战，需要保护感知隐私。

Method: 将天线阵列和导频子载波视为空域和频域采样机制，通过空-频欠采样使MIMO模糊函数发生畸变，推导闭式表达式，分析虚假目标，建立定位模糊的充分条件，证明足够大的子采样因子下非授权接收者必然将目标定位在错误位置。

Result: 理论推导和数值实验验证了子采样框架在不损害合法ISAC性能的前提下，通过混叠欺骗实现感知和定位隐私保护。

Conclusion: 稀疏阵列和稀疏导频能自然实现感知和定位隐私，为ISAC系统提供了一种低开销的安全增强方案。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) enables simultaneous communication and sensing using shared spectrum and hardware resources in wireless systems. However, securing the sensing functionality against unauthorized receivers remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we propose a sub-sampling based sensing-privacy framework for communication-centric (CC)-ISAC systems that jointly exploits sparse arrays and sparse pilot allocations to induce controlled aliasing in the spatial and frequency domains, respectively. By interpreting antenna arrays and pilot subcarriers as spatial and frequency sampling mechanisms, respectively, we show that spatial-frequency undersampling naturally distorts the range-angle multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) ambiguity function (AF) observed by an unauthorized receiver. To this end, we first derive a closed-form expression for the range-angle MIMO-AF, and subsequently characterize the ghost targets that arise due to spatial and frequency-domain aliasing. Next, we establish a sufficient condition under which these ambiguities jointly translate into positioning ambiguity and show that, for sufficiently large spatial and frequency sub-sampling factors, an unauthorized receiver inevitably positions a target at incorrect ghost positions. Finally, we show that the proposed sub-sampling framework preserves the native legitimate ISAC performance without introducing additional trade-offs. Numerical results verify the analysis and show that sparse arrays and sparse pilots naturally enable sensing and positioning privacy through deception by aliasing.

</details>


### [262] [Token Communication for Multimodal Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07279)
*Jingkai Ying,Zhijin Qin,Yuan Shen,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的token通信框架，通过在视觉tokenizer中集成神经编解码器来减少传输数据量，同时保持多模态理解性能。该方法采用双阶段视觉-语言语义对齐训练策略，并在不同传输数据量条件下表现出优于其他图像处理方案的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Transformer架构和MLLMs的广泛应用，token已成为信息处理的基本单元。在MLLMs中，视觉和文本信息均以token形式表示和处理。随着MLLMs的快速发展部署，token的高效传输变得日益重要。需要解决如何在与MLLMs交互过程中减少传输数据量的同时，保持其多模态理解性能的问题。

Method: 1. 在视觉tokenizer中集成神经编解码器以控制传输比特数
2. 接收端解码后的潜变量通过两条路径处理：解码器重建图像作为重建先验，适配器将潜变量转换为视觉token并注入视觉tokenizer的中间层
3. 设计双阶段视觉-语言语义对齐训练方案：首先通过蒸馏损失预热适配器，然后通过对齐损失与文本语义进行对齐
4. 通过特征级线性调制引入自适应适配器，使单个适配器支持多种编解码器速率

Result: 在各种MLLM基准上的大量仿真结果表明，在相同传输数据量条件下，所提方案比MLLMs的其他图像处理方案取得了更好的任务性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的token通信框架能够在保持或提升多模态理解性能的同时，有效减少MLLMs交互过程中的数据传输量。通过神经编解码器集成、双阶段语义对齐训练和自适应适配器设计，实现了对多种编解码器速率的支持，展现出相比现有方法的优势。

Abstract: With the broad success of the Transformer architecture, token is becoming a new basic information processing unit. This trend is especially evident in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), where both visual and textual information are represented and processed as tokens. With the rapid deployment of MLLMs, the efficient transmission of tokens has become increasingly important. This paper investigates how to reduce the amount of transmitted data during interactions with MLLMs while preserving their multimodal understanding performance. To address this problem, we propose a token communication framework tailored to MLLMs. In the proposed framework, a neural codec is integrated into the vision tokenizer to control the number of transmitted bits. At the receiver, the decoded latents are processed through two paths. The decoder reconstructs image as a reconstruction prior, while the adapter converts latents into visual tokens and injects them into an intermediate layer of the vision tokenizer. To make the injected tokens suitable for MLLMs, we further design a two-stage visual-language semantic alignment training scheme. The adapter is first warmed up by a distillation loss and then aligned with textual semantics through an alignment loss. An adaptive adapter is also introduced through feature-wise linear modulation, allowing one adapter to support multiple codec rates. Extensive simulations on various MLLM benchmarks show that, under the same amount of transmitted data, the proposed scheme achieves better task performance than other image processing schemes for MLLMs.

</details>


### [263] [RIS-Aided mmWave Localization Under Cross-Link Interference via Beam-Domain ML Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07444)
*Md Tarek Hassan,Dmitry Zelenchuk,Muhammad Ali Babar Abbasi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需CSI的波束域指纹框架，利用RIS反射状态下的接收SNR映射UE方位角与距离，并扩展至干扰场景下SINR指纹，通过INR约束校准保持干扰可解释性。仿真显示KNN在清洁条件下角度MAE 0.37°、距离MAE 4cm；干扰下角度MAE升至1.4°、距离MAE 7.6cm。关键发现：干扰对角估计的劣化远大于距离估计，源于波束域指纹中位置信息的非对称编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RIS辅助的毫米波6G网络中，当基站与用户设备直接链路不可用时，精确UE定位对于波束管理至关重要。现有方法通常需要信道状态信息(CSI)，而RIS系统可能难以获取CSI。此外，实际环境中存在交叉链路干扰(CLI)，会污染指纹信号，降低定位精度。因此需要一种无需CSI且鲁棒于干扰的定位方法。

Method: 提出波束域指纹框架，将一组预定义RIS反射状态下接收的SNR映射到UE方位角和距离，无需CSI。进一步扩展至干扰场景，将SNR指纹转化为SINR指纹，并采用干扰噪声比(INR)约束的校准策略，确保干扰水平物理可解释。评估四种机器学习回归器（如KNN、RF、SVM、ANN）在清洁与干扰条件下的性能。仿真采用28 GHz频段、20x20 RIS。

Result: 在清洁条件下，KNN实现最低角度平均绝对误差(MAE)0.37°和距离MAE 4cm。在干扰条件下，角度MAE升至1.4°，距离MAE升至7.6cm。所有模型均显示干扰对角估计的劣化远大于距离估计。

Conclusion: 波束域指纹框架可在无CSI情况下实现高精度UE定位，但干扰会显著影响角度估计。通过INR约束校准可管理干扰影响。非对称编码导致角度更敏感，未来工作可优化指纹设计以提升抗干扰能力。

Abstract: Accurate user equipment (UE) localization is critical for beam management in reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted millimeter-wave (mmWave) based sixth-generation (6G) networks, especially if the direct base-station-UE links are unavailable. This paper proposes a beam-domain fingerprint framework that maps the received signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) across a small set of predefined RIS reflection states to the UE azimuth angle and range, without requiring channel state information (CSI). Crucially, we extend the framework to a realistic interference-impaired scenario in which a nearby cross-link interferer (CLI) corrupts the clean SNR fingerprint, yielding a signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) fingerprint; an interference-to-noise ratio (INR)-constrained calibration strategy keeps the interference level physically interpretable. Four machine-learning (ML) regressors are evaluated under both conditions. Simulation results at 28 GHz with a 20x20 RIS show that k-nearest neighbors (KNN) achieves the lowest angle MAE of 0.37 degrees and range MAE of 4 cm under clean conditions, rising to 1.4 degrees and 7.6 cm under interference. A key finding is that interference degrades angle estimation substantially more than range estimation across all models, a consequence of the asymmetric encoding of location information in the beam-domain fingerprint.

</details>
